<!-- msime-download-guide --> ## 下载  大多数人下载第一行的**水杉输入法**（`msime-macos-0.58.0-universal.pkg`）就行：它是完整版，包含全部输入方案。其他版本只带其中一部分方案，可以和完整版同时安装。每个安装包都同时支持 Apple 芯片和 Intel 芯片的 Mac。  | 版本 | 安装包 | Homebrew | | --- | --- | --- | | **水杉输入法**（完整版，推荐） | [msime-macos-0.58.0-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.58.0/msime-macos-0.58.0-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime` | | 水杉五笔 | [msime-macos-wubi-0.58.0-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.58.0/msime-macos-wubi-0.58.0-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-wubi` | | 水杉拼音 | [msime-macos-pinyin-0.58.0-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.58.0/msime-macos-pinyin-0.58.0-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-pinyin` | | 水杉日语 | [msime-macos-japanese-0.58.0-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.58.0/msime-macos-japanese-0.58.0-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-japanese` | | 水杉越南语 | [msime-macos-vietnamese-0.58.0-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.58.0/msime-macos-vietnamese-0.58.0-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-vietnamese` | | 水杉藏文 | [msime-macos-tibetan-0.58.0-universal.pkg](https://github.com/metasequoiaime/msime/releases/download/macos-v0.58.0/msime-macos-tibetan-0.58.0-universal.pkg) | `brew install --cask metasequoiaime/tap/msime-tibetan` |  双击下载的安装包，按提示安装（需要管理员密码）。装好后应用会自动打开，点「立即安装」；第一次在这台 Mac 上安装时，请退出登录再重新登录，然后到「系统设置」→「键盘」→「文字输入」→「输入法」中添加。`SHA256SUMS` 是这些安装包的 SHA-256 校验值。 <!-- /msime-download-guide -->  ## v0.58.0 更新内容  ### 🧠 整句选词：长句和聊天打字更准  - **逐键模型参与整句解码**：开启「增强」后，整句解码时就让逐键模型参与评分，正确的整句不再在前几列被提前剪掉。比如「下周四上线」不会再被「下周寺上线」挤出候选。开着全拼纠错时，「看个画展」也不会被打成「抗饿画展」。(#10759, #10764) - **新的按键重排模型**：模型换成用聊天上文训练的版本，在聊天式输入里更贴近日常说法。会话开头单打 `ma` 这类单字时，改为比较「这个字单独说完」的可能性，避免「妈」被「麻」「马」顶掉。(#10811) - 大模型一次只来得及比较 9 条整句时，改用小模型比较完整的 32 条，正确答案更容易进入比较范围。大模型本来就能比较更多条的机器不受影响。(#10732) - 一句话开头不再首选句尾语气词。比如消息开头单打 `ma` 首选「妈」而不是「吗」，`ba` 首选「爸」而不是「吧」。没有正在输入的内容时按回车，会在上文里记一个换行，以便区分一条新消息从哪里开始。「啊、呀、哦、哇」这类常放在句首的叹词不受影响。(#10737) - 单独成词的轻声助词「的 / 地 / 得 / 了 / 着」也算一份成词加分，只在读音对得上时生效。比如「那我买周六早上的高铁票了」不再被打成「…高铁飘了」，「中午只能吃食堂了」不再变成「…食躺了」。(#10745) - 逐键模型只在和首选字数相同的读法之间调整顺序。比如「要上线」不再被打成「要上西安」，「今晚上线」也一样。(#10734) - 停顿约 300 ms 后，不再用较弱的旧模型把逐键排好的顺序重新换掉，打字时看到的排序就是最终顺序。(#10742) - 字表之外的生僻字不再因为模型的未知字评分而占便宜。比如「饭别做太多」不再上屏成「𫔍别做太多」，「别什么都瞒着我」不再变成「癟什么都瞒着我」。(#10753)  ### ⚡ 性能  - 上屏之后，新一段输入的第一个键不再卡一下。此前这一下要现场编码整段上文，约 9 ms，机器忙时约 18 ms；现在放到后台完成。(#10726) - 候选窗每次按键重建时，不再白白计算按钮尺寸。此前这一步占了构建候选窗的大部分时间，`build_ms` 中位数约 21 ms。(修复 #10727，#10774)  ### 🐛 问题修复  **语音**  - 修复输入法刚启动时，麦克风按钮偶尔不显示、语音快捷键没有反应，要重新点进输入框才恢复的问题。现在首次激活或首次通过按键取得焦点时，会先恢复语音和工具栏设置，再显示工具栏。工具栏被关闭时也不会再留下一个点了没反应的麦克风。(感谢 @xcv58，#10637) - 按住右 Option 说话期间，再按 F10、字母这类组合键，会取消这次按住开始的录音，松开后不再把识别结果上屏。此前使用 HTTP 提供商时，组合键不会取消录音。(修复 #10580，#10704)  **输入**  - 修复装好新构建或重启输入法后的头几秒里，按键丢失或被原样插进文字的问题，比如打出 `ndehuodongyezhunb` 这样的字母串。逐键重排模型现在改在后台加载，加载完成前照常打字，只是暂不重排，加载好后在下一次输入空闲时自动生效。(修复 #10743，#10752)  ### 🔧 开发与构建  - 在 macOS 上运行 `ctest` 时，所有测试默认使用构建目录里的独立主目录，不再改动开发机上正在使用的输入法偏好。(#10750) - 放宽崩溃符号测试的超时上限到 60 秒，避免 x86_64 的 macOS nightly 因超时变红。(修复 #10808，#10812)  ### 🙏 感谢  - 贡献者：   - @xcv58：修复启动后语音输入偶尔不可用  <!-- msime-build {"run":41760316575,"source":"532bd46fd4db97b85567accb4fd30245f4fd1bb5","channel":"stable"} --> 
```

### 2026-11-04T01:55:06Z [**t8y2/motty**](https://github.com/t8y2/motty)

```
## 新功能  - 工作区：把多台主机的终端组合保存为工作区，一次打开，也可以存成模板再编辑；终端按网格排列。 - 远程进程管理补全：可以列出远程进程并查看 PID、父进程、用户、状态、CPU 和内存，面板可开成独立窗口。 - AI 设置可以逐个或批量「测速」模型端点，只列出可用于对话或推理的模型；批量探测可设置并发数和超时。 - 快捷键可以绑定任意按键或组合：单键、标点、空格、导航键、F1–F12，搭配任意修饰键。 - 会话日志可以排序和筛选，并记住上次的视图。 - 新建标签的主机选择可以按最近登录排序，并显示「刚刚」「N 秒前」这类时间。 - 后台终端输出提醒：标签标题用颜色提示未查看的输出——蓝色表示输出中，绿色表示结束或静默，黄色表示命令失败。 - 导入时可以保存导出文件里可读取的明文 SSH 密码，导入前会列出数量并确认，且不覆盖已有密码；MobaXterm 文件编码有歧义时可以选择 UTF-8 或 GB18030。  ## 破坏性变更  - 快捷键现在可以占用几乎任何按键，已绑定的按键会先触发快捷键，再到终端。默认绑定没有变化，只有你新增的绑定才会挡住程序。 - macOS 上，没有绑定到 Motty 功能的 Command 组合，在程序启用 kitty 键盘协议后会转发给程序，不再被 Motty 丢弃。  ## 改进  - 终端按字符宽度成批写入，并对连续相同样式只解析一次，滚动和大量输出更快。  ## 修复  - 一个窗口在另一个窗口保存过之后会一直保存失败的问题已修复：现在会重新读取共享记录再保存。 - 重连后的 shell 不再继承上一个程序的 kitty 键盘模式。 - 主机标签的下拉选项不再被表单边界截断。 - 跳板主机与目标主机的下拉框可以用滚轮滚动。  ## 安装  | 平台 | 包 | | --- | --- | | macOS（arm64、amd64） | `.dmg`、`.zip` | | Windows（amd64、arm64） | 安装程序、便携版 zip（数据在程序旁的 `motty-data`） | | Linux（amd64、arm64） | `.deb`、`.tar.gz` |   Built against vivaui/viva `76cd6f82c7c948fd4dbec4f7cc8ef7aee1a2d119`. 
```

```
This one cleans up the band. On Team, Enterprise and managed machines the warning on your first prompt is gone, and the band shows your model and session name again. In VS Code it stays on one line, other plugins' commands no longer print under ponytail's name, and the noisy "duplicate?" hint is gone.   ## What's Changed * fix(band): show the level again after /clear and /resume by @timofeevAV in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1161 * fix(map): raise the git ls-files buffer so large repos keep tracked files by @HyperionBurn in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1156 * ci: run agentic benchmark selftest in test workflow by @ml-lubich in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1154 * docs: clarify Cursor mode state scope by @Boulea7 in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1135 * fix(codex): small composer icon instead of the 554 KB logo by @DietrichGebert in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1164 * fix(hooks): 15 s hook timeout so a slow node start on Windows keeps its output by @DietrichGebert in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1165 * fix(hooks): give the codebase map 3 s so a slow Windows start keeps it by @DietrichGebert in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1166 * fix(bench): wait for the killed todo server before the workdir cleanup by @devohmycode in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1116 * fix(band): find the transcript without a classic hook by @trhinehart-attentive in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1242 * fix(band): watch only /clear, /resume and /branch, so other plugins' output is not labelled ponytail by @ph3on1x in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1291 * fix(band): size the row to bodyColumns only in the terminal by @lelmarir in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1302 * fix(band): drop the duplicate? hint, count only project files, tighter details view by @DietrichGebert in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1305 * chore: release v5.3.3 by @DietrichGebert in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1307  ## New Contributors * @timofeevAV made their first contribution in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1161 * @HyperionBurn made their first contribution in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1156 * @ml-lubich made their first contribution in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1154 * @trhinehart-attentive made their first contribution in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1242 * @ph3on1x made their first contribution in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1291 * @lelmarir made their first contribution in https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/pull/1302  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/DietrichGebert/ponytail/compare/v5.3.2...v5.3.3

### 2026-11-01T03:46:24Z [**since2006/shell-rs**](https://github.com/since2006/shell-rs)

```
### Shared core for mobile  This release moves the desktop app's SSH connections, SFTP transfers, host and credential management, database persistence and cloud sync into Rust libraries independent of the interface. The desktop app now uses these shared cores, and Swift and Kotlin bindings let future iOS and Android clients use the same authentication, resumable transfers and end-to-end encrypted sync. The shared cores and mobile bindings are MIT licensed; the desktop app remains GPL-3.0-only.  ### Added  - App lock: require a password at startup, lock automatically after inactivity, and choose whether the external CLI remains available while locked. The lock settings and password verifier are kept in the system keychain. - On Windows, “Minimize to tray on close” under Settings → General → Window keeps connections, transfers and port forwards running after the window is closed. Restore the window or quit from the tray. Off by default. ([#83](https://github.com/since2006/shell-rs/issues/83)) - Choose the terminal cursor's shape and whether it blinks under Settings → Terminal → Interaction. - “Duplicate session” in a terminal tab's menu carries over its title and, for SSH terminals that report it, its working directory.  ### Improved  - The left sidebar remembers whether it was shown or hidden across launches. - Open saved hosts directly from the title bar's Quick connect button. The sidebar toggle follows the panel's edge when resized, and the panel stays at least as wide as its default.  ### Fixed  - On macOS, waking from sleep or switching displays could crash ShellRS while the screen was temporarily unavailable. - On Windows without the Visual C++ Redistributable, such as Windows 11 LTSC, ShellRS didn’t start: “VCRUNTIME140.dll was not found”. 
```

## Bug Fixes  - Fixed an issue where a plugin hook's `auth.set` could sign in an account that had been signed out while the hook was running.  ### Install  Download from [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai), or `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`. An installed magpie updates itself.  - **macOS (Apple Silicon):** `magpie-darwin-arm64.dmg` - **macOS (Intel):** `magpie-darwin-amd64.dmg` - **Windows:** `magpie-windows-amd64.exe` (ARM: `magpie-windows-arm64.exe`) - **Linux:** `magpie-linux-amd64` / `magpie-linux-arm64` (needs GTK 3 and WebKitGTK 4.1) - **Terminal only:** `magpie-cli-<os>-<arch>`  <!-- lang:zh -->  ## Bug 修复  - 修复了一个问题：插件钩子的 `auth.set` 可能会登录一个在钩子运行期间已被注销的账户。  ### 安装  从 [usemagpie.ai](https://usemagpie.ai) 下载，或运行 `curl -fsSL https://usemagpie.ai/install.sh | sh`。已安装的 magpie 会自动更新。    --- The same release, and the feed magpie updates from: https://github.com/yetone/magpie-releases/releases/tag/v0.1.2899 
```

## NyaTerm v2.0.1  `v2.0.1` 是 NyaTerm 2.0 正式版后的首个维护更新。本次重点完善 **云同步、Windows RDP、SSH 身份验证、终端输入及 SFTP 文件操作**，同时加入若干实用交互能力，并修复多项影响日常使用的问题。  ### ✨ 新增功能  - **Cloud Sync V3 预览**：在云同步设置中提供可选的新同步方式，首批支持 **WebDAV、S3 和本地目录**。与旧快照同步协议并存，选择后需要先保存配置，再生成并确认同步预览。   - 连接、分组、代理、隧道、快捷命令、笔记、图标和主题等资源可按稳定 ID 合并；不同字段的修改可自动合并，同一字段冲突则显示候选供手动选择，并允许先应用无冲突部分。   - 外观偏好、快捷键、AI 非敏感配置均有独立同步范围；密码、私钥、OTP、AI 密钥等敏感数据以及 SSH/VNC 主机信任信息需要**单独启用**，不会默认同步或自动信任远端身份。   - 首次确认会生成并验证加密安全备份；同步发布失败时保留待发布状态，增加主密码轮换及数据回读校验机制。当前仍属**预览能力**，并非所有旧云存储提供方都支持新协议。 - **Windows 原生 RDP 连接方式**：新增 Windows 原生 RDP 后端，并为显式选择的 ActiveX 连接采用独立辅助进程，减少不同远程桌面会话之间的相互干扰。 - **终端 MAC 地址快捷操作**：识别终端输出中的 MAC 地址，支持多种常见格式之间的转换，并直接复制或插入转换结果。关联 Issue #1079。 - **外部链接与启动参数**：扩展 SSH、Telnet、本地终端的外部调用，支持 SSH 的终端或 SFTP 会话模式、复用窗口参数，以及同名已保存连接的选择。Windows 安装版增加 `nyaterm://`、`ssh://`、`telnet://` 协议注册与卸载清理。 - **WindTerm 加密会话导入**：遇到受主密码保护的 WindTerm 自动登录信息时，可在导入流程中输入主密码；补充解密失败和凭据不完整时的提示。 - **终端命令建议填充**：允许在确认输入状态安全时补全当前命令，避免盲目替换已有内容，尤其改善网络设备命令联想重复的问题。关联 Issue #1055。  ### 🐛 问题修复  - **RDP 连接与显示**：修复连接后端选择无法正确保存的问题；按实际面板尺寸发起适配窗口的连接，减少初次连接后重复调整分辨率；改进原生 RDP 在不同 DPI 下的缩放表现。 - **RDP 认证与恢复**：关闭远程身份验证时不再错误要求密码（PR #1042）；仅针对适合重试的网络故障自动重连，并支持复制失败画面的错误信息（PR #1006）。 - **RDP 多会话**：修复部分会话实例互相影响、窗口还原及 ActiveX 显示配置不受支持时的异常处理。 - **VNC 大画面更新**：修复包含大量合法更新矩形的帧被错误判为协议故障的问题，同时保留必要的资源边界校验（PR #1047，关联 Issue #1045）。 - **VNC 无密码连接**：修复无需密码的自动协商和重试仍反复索要密码的问题；改善真正缺少凭据时的提示，并解释经典 VNC 密码的长度限制（PR #1047、PR #1062，关联 Issue #1046）。 - **首次云同步冲突**：新安装或清空数据的设备首次拉取时，不再把空本地内容与云端已有内容误判为冲突；云端仅有空快照时也会保留本机数据并允许正常推送。双方均有实际内容的冲突仍须处理（PR #1057，关联 Issue #1051）。 - **SFTP 文本写入**：修复向远端写入文本时新文件创建、已有文件覆盖不正确的问题，涉及 `sftp_write_text`（PR #1027）。 - **终端键盘与选择**：修复 `Ctrl+-`、`Ctrl+Shift+-` 未按预期发送 `^_` 的问题（PR #1044）；改善跨行选择、完整缓冲区选择与软换行边界处理。 - **快捷键录制**：修复录制快捷键时无法正确捕获已经绑定的组合键的问题（PR #1077）。 - **关闭与更新**：修复启用关闭到托盘后退出行为异常、可能妨碍应用更新的问题；关闭窗口或退出时更妥善地处理未保存的设置草稿（PR #1011）。 - **MCP 工具结果**：修复 `sftp_list` 的 `structuredContent` 格式，以及工具列表遗漏 `ttlMs`、`cacheScope` 缓存字段的问题（PR #1026、PR #1075）。  ### ⚡ 性能与稳定性优化  - **SSH 运行时认证**：改善连接时补充或重新输入凭据的流程，强化密码输入、显示切换、空白字符保留与校验反馈，减少保存连接与临时认证之间的交互混淆。 - **批量文件冲突处理**：上传重复文件时可对**本次上传批次**的后续冲突选择统一覆盖，其他上传任务仍独立确认；同时优化传输任务排队、删除任务和文件元数据处理。 - **应用关闭时的同步安全**：退出与更新前协调正在执行的同步操作，避免同步写入在关闭过程中被直接中断。 - **终端输出整理**：优化控制序列与最近输出文本的处理，使传入 AI、MCP 和终端操作菜单的文本更加准确。 - **崩溃排查**：增加本地崩溃报告记录和旧报告清理，方便定位异常退出原因。  ### ♻️ 重构与优化  - **同步数据处理**：引入更细粒度的同步数据模型、加密对象与合并流程，为多端配置同步和冲突恢复提供基础；不将其等同于所有云存储服务都已升级。 - **外部包管理器适配**：源码构建者可通过 `NYATERM_PACKAGE_MANAGER` 声明由外部包管理器负责更新；此类构建会关闭应用内更新检查及入口，并在“关于”界面显示包管理器名称。普通官方安装包保持原有更新行为（PR #1066）。  ### 🎨 界面与交互优化  - **设置融入工作区**：设置从独立模态窗口改为工作区标签页，减少配置操作与终端会话之间的切换阻碍。 - **会话标签操作**：调整新建标签和标签操作菜单，完善重命名、复制、断开连接以及操作后的焦点恢复。 - **AI 模型选择**：输入模型名称时优先显示匹配项，并改善已添加模型的展示与选择（PR #1074）。 - **文件传输与远程管理**：传输面板增加进入文件管理器的入口；优化远程进程信息、监控面板以及窄窗口下的布局。 - **窗口与托盘**：改善标题栏、连接编辑器和工作区布局；优化 macOS 托盘图标的模板显示。  ### 📚 文档与国际化  - 补充外部协议调用、SSH 会话模式、云同步预览与外部包管理器构建说明。 - 完善 SSH 身份验证、主密码导入、文件权限、连接取消等提示的中、英、法、日、韩及繁体中文文案。  ### 👥 贡献者  - @HarpeLm：修复 RDP 无认证密码提示、SFTP 文本写入、终端快捷键、MCP 响应及快捷键录制问题（PR #1042、PR #1027、PR #1044、PR #1026、PR #1075、PR #1077） - @Coflys：优化 RDP 网络故障重连策略与失败错误复制（PR #1006） - @hualong1009：修复 VNC 大量更新矩形被误判和无密码重试索要密码的问题（PR #1047） - @moonmicah：修复首次云同步空内容误判为冲突的问题（PR #1057） - @sgnay：完善外部包管理器接管应用更新的支持（PR #1066） - @maxim-palshin：完善经典 VNC 密码长度限制提示（PR #1062） - @llyVictory：优化 AI 设置中模型名称匹配与候选排序（PR #1074） - @HIUEETR：修复关闭窗口时最小化到托盘的行为（PR #1011） - @work1024：参与 Windows 原生 RDP 后端、设置标签页及远程桌面显示改进（直接提交）  ### ⚠️ 升级说明  - **Cloud Sync V3 是可选预览功能**：建议先保留现有备份，再手动启用并检查同步预览。新旧同步空间并存；敏感数据与主机信任默认不纳入同步。切换主密码或迁移其他设备前，请阅读同步说明。 - **Windows 协议关联**：安装版可能注册 SSH/Telnet 链接处理器，系统仍可要求用户选择默认应用；便携版不会主动注册。包含密码的 SSH URL 可能被浏览器或外部调用工具记录，请谨慎使用。 - **外部包管理器安装**：若构建时启用了包管理器接管更新，后续更新应通过相应包管理器完成；官方标准构建不受影响。  ### 📦 版本信息  - **版本**：`v2.0.1` - **发布类型**：Stable Release - **目标分支**：`main` - **变更范围**：`v2.0.0...v2.0.1` 
```

### 2026-11-02T07:52:05Z [**ejfkdev/ddc**](https://github.com/ejfkdev/ddc)

v0.1.35  Fully static musl Linux builds, plus install and CI coverage for them.  - New release assets: x86_64-unknown-linux-musl and   aarch64-unknown-linux-musl. These are fully static (no dynamic NEEDED   entries), so they run on Alpine and any other musl distribution. - The npm wrapper detects musl at install time and picks the matching   static asset; cargo-binstall resolves the musl target triples from the   same raw-binary naming. - CI builds and smoke-tests the x86_64 musl target on every push and   asserts the binary is not dynamically linked. - README install docs list the new musl binaries; the generated npm   package description follows whichever target set the release contains. - This release keeps the v0.1.34 quality-first baseline: javac parse gate,   full-classpath semantic battery, cure's exact simplification, and the   dependency-closure getclass path.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ejfkdev/ddc/compare/v0.1.34...v0.1.35 
```

# Vetta 0.5.71 <!-- release-status: unreleased -->  ## 改进  - 首次进入、还没有项目和对话时，左侧不再显示项目区（含筛选抬头），而是在对话区下方显示「从这里开始」卡片，提供「开始新对话」和「新建项目」两个入口。 - 首次启动的引导页切换步骤、点击按钮时更跟手：去掉了切换时的模糊滤镜，步骤指示和按钮改用更轻的样式动画，减少毛玻璃窗口下的卡顿。 - 侧边栏「从这里开始」卡片里的两个按钮改用统一的按钮样式，圆角跟随主题设置，不再固定。 - 移除「场景」功能：左侧导航的「场景」入口与场景页已删除，对话里不再有场景选择、`@scene:` 引用与由场景生成的锁定待办；能力页、命令面板、智能体能力配置里也不再出现场景类型。已装在 `~/.vetta/scene` 下的场景文件不会被删除，但不再被加载；旧对话里的场景引用按技能显示，市场索引里的场景条目会被忽略。  ## 修复  - 修复智能体不信任后台任务的问题：运行耗时命令时，它不再额外再开一个命令轮询或 sleep 等待结果，而是直接等任务结束时的自动通知。 - 修复 UI 设计的交互模式下，「跟随设计稿」会给灵动岛稿子套上刘海屏外壳的问题：稿子尺寸恰好是 iPhone 14（390 × 844）时，外壳现在按稿子的状态栏设置（灵动岛 / 刘海）选择，不再只看尺寸。  ## 其他  - 内部清理：coding-agent、插件 SDK、主题组件与相关文档里 scene 类型的接口一并移除（Todo 的锁定机制保留，知识库加工仍在使用）。 
```

# Pebrel 2.4.0  ## English  macOS, Linux, and Android downloads are Preview builds.  ### Added  - Add streaming SFTP previews for remote UTF-8 files. - Add SFTP editing and conflict checks for UTF-8 files up to 8 MiB. - Add progressive image previews with progress, cancel, and retry. - Add Luau configuration and plugin commands, with legacy filenames still recognized. - Add font, opacity, tab, and pane defaults in `pebrel.luau`. - Add GLSL background and terminal effects alongside WGSL. - Add shader controls for animation speed, frame rate, and parameters. - Add opt-in next-message suggestions for supported local Claude Code, Pi, and OpenCode sessions. - Add a separate provider and model for next-message suggestions. - Add native click-to-place and drag selection in supported Bash, Zsh, and PowerShell input. - Add directly transmitted, uncompressed RGB/RGBA images via the Kitty graphics protocol. ([#767](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/767)) - Add SFTP Name and Size columns, a full-height file list, and a status bar. - Add copy and download actions on SFTP file rows. - Add file and folder uploads plus remote copy and paste in SFTP. - Add Ctrl/Shift selection and batch downloads in SFTP. - Add drag-out downloads from the SFTP list. - Add Japanese translations for settings help and option labels. ([#793](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/793))  ### Fixed  - Fix SFTP preview opening for PNG, JPEG, WebP, BMP, and GIF first frames. Addresses [#861](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/861). - Fix Codex permission requests showing a busy spinner. - Fix misaligned activity icons in the sidebar and top tabs. - Fix explicit agent states being overwritten when full Hooks are available. - Fix generic notifications incorrectly showing the input-request indicator. - Fix OpenCode permission and question replies, and busy and retry indicators. - Fix OpenCode idle events being mistaken for success. - Fix agent Hook routing after reattaching supported SSH sessions. - Fix SSH task titles falling out of sync with the active pane after reattaching. - Fix duplicate CLI spinners and progress symbols in AI task titles. - Fix native pointer editing with remapped PSReadLine chords in Windows PowerShell. - Fix duplicate tabs when the running process receives a launch but its reply is lost. Addresses [#797](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/797). - Fix localhost proxying with an empty bypass list, including OpenCode startup. Addresses [#807](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/807). - Fix a stale cursor crash during terminal resize or selection. Addresses [#788](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/788). - Fix the welcome command in fish with or without Fastfetch. Addresses [#803](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/803). - Fix workspace shortcuts while an image tab has focus. Addresses [#774](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/774). - Fix search for Japanese settings and localized shortcut actions. Addresses [#821](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/821). - Fix nested paths being cut off after native Tab completion. Addresses [#676](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/676). - Fix workspace and agent session restoration after Windows shutdown. Addresses [#792](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/792). - Fix MSYS2 Zsh discovery and shell detection for imported executables. Addresses [#812](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/812). - Fix false busy indicators for ordinary full-screen terminal tools. Addresses [#805](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/805). - Fix immediate disconnects on Windows SSH hosts that cannot run POSIX startup integration. Addresses [#810](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/810). - Fix Shift+Enter so it inserts a newline in recognized CodeBuddy sessions. Addresses [#839](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/839). - Fix shell and profile ID instructions in CLI help, completions, and docs. Addresses [#665](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/665). - Fix unbounded X11 input method property names in long Linux sessions. ([#668](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/668)) - Fix missing Hermes artwork and incorrect agent icon colors. ([#832](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/832)) - Fix cramped hover spacing on the Project and Support entries. Addresses [#859](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/859). - Fix small click targets for the Clear and Restore keymap buttons. ([#644](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/644))  ## 中文  macOS、Linux 和 Android 下载包为预览版。  ### 新增  - 新增 SFTP 远端 UTF-8 文件的分块预览。 - 新增 8 MiB 以内 UTF-8 文件的远端编辑和保存冲突检查。 - 新增 SFTP 图片渐进预览，支持查看进度、取消和重试。 - 新增 Luau 配置和插件命令，仍识别旧配置文件名。 - 新增 `pebrel.luau` 字体、透明度、标签和窗格默认设置。 - 新增 GLSL 背景与终端效果，兼容原有 WGSL。 - 新增着色器的动画速度、帧率和参数设置。 - 新增受支持的本地 Claude Code、Pi 和 OpenCode 会话的下一句建议，默认关闭。 - 新增下一句建议专用的服务商和模型设置。 - 新增受支持的 Bash、Zsh 和 PowerShell 原生输入点击定位和拖选。 - 新增 Kitty 图形协议直接传输未压缩 RGB/RGBA 图片的支持。 ([#767](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/767)) - 新增 SFTP 名称和大小列、全高文件列表及底部状态栏。 - 新增 SFTP 文件行的复制和下载按钮。 - 新增 SFTP 文件和文件夹上传，以及远端复制粘贴。 - 新增 SFTP Ctrl/Shift 多选和批量下载。 - 新增从 SFTP 列表拖出文件下载。 - 新增设置帮助和选项标签的日语翻译。 ([#793](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/793))  ### 修复  - 修复 SFTP 无法打开 PNG、JPEG、WebP、BMP 和 GIF 首帧预览的问题。对应 [#861](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/861)。 - 修复 Codex 申请权限时错误显示转圈的问题。 - 修复侧栏和顶部标签的活动图标偏移。 - 修复完整 Hook 上报的 Agent 状态被推断覆盖的问题。 - 修复普通通知错误显示等待输入手掌的问题。 - 修复 OpenCode 权限和问答回复，以及忙碌和重试状态。 - 修复将 OpenCode 空闲事件误判为成功的问题。 - 修复重新挂接受支持的 SSH 会话后 Agent Hook 路由失效的问题。 - 修复 SSH 会话重新挂接后任务标题没有跟随活动窗格的问题。 - 修复 AI 任务标题重复显示 CLI 转圈和进度符号的问题。 - 修复 Windows PowerShell 更换 PSReadLine 组合键后原生鼠标编辑失效的问题。 - 修复驻留进程收到启动请求但响应丢失时重复开标签的问题。对应 [#797](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/797)。 - 修复绕过列表为空时本地请求走代理的问题，包括 OpenCode 启动。对应 [#807](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/807)。 - 修复终端缩放或选区操作时因光标过期引发的崩溃。对应 [#788](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/788)。 - 修复 fish 中的欢迎命令，兼容有无 Fastfetch 的情况。对应 [#803](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/803)。 - 修复图片标签获得焦点时工作区快捷键失效的问题。对应 [#774](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/774)。 - 修复日语设置和本地化快捷键动作的搜索。对应 [#821](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/821)。 - 修复原生 Tab 补全后嵌套路径被截断的问题。对应 [#676](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/676)。 - 修复 Windows 关机后工作区和 Agent 会话无法完整恢复的问题。对应 [#792](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/792)。 - 修复 MSYS2 Zsh 自动发现及手动导入时的 Shell 识别。对应 [#812](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/812)。 - 修复普通全屏终端工具错误显示忙碌转圈的问题。对应 [#805](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/805)。 - 修复不支持 POSIX 启动集成的 Windows SSH 主机连接后立即断开的问题。对应 [#810](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/810)。 - 修复已识别的 CodeBuddy 会话中 Shift+Enter 无法换行的问题。对应 [#839](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/839)。 - 修复 CLI 帮助、补全和文档中的 Shell 与配置 ID 说明。对应 [#665](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/665)。 - 修复 Linux 长时间运行时 X11 输入法属性名称持续累积的问题。 ([#668](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/668)) - 修复 Hermes 图标缺失和部分 Agent 图标颜色错误的问题。 ([#832](https://github.com/Kuddev/pebrel/pull/832)) - 修复“项目与支持”入口悬停背景左右留白不足的问题。对应 [#859](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/859)。 - 修复按键映射页“清除”和“恢复默认”按钮的点击区域过小的问题。 ### 2026-10-29T23:27:36Z [**anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop**](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop)

```
# 中文  本次 Next 更新将上游升级至 DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.4，优化应用启动速度与窗口状态恢复，升级恢复助手的 Profile 修复、撤销与故障报告能力，支持 `dshfind` 插件索引，并将内置 Agents Anywhere 插件升级至 2.1.0（支持 Codex 会话交接与双端轮流对话、依赖环境重装及历史消息顺序修复）。  DSH NEXT 是社区维护的开源项目，并非 DeepSeek 官方产品。  **上游版本：** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.4](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.1-rc.4)。  ## 新增与改进  - **启动速度与窗口体验:** 加快 macOS 无系统代理时的网络探测与启动环境变量加载，并启用前端脚本编译缓存以提升二次启动速度；自动记忆并恢复主窗口的位置、尺寸与最大化状态。 - **恢复助手与 Profile 修复:** “备份并修复当前 Profile”现会先创建完整备份，将 Profile 重置为默认模板插件并重新进入首次设置引导，同时在“快速恢复”页新增“撤销上次修复”支持一键还原；打开恢复助手时自动生成单文件故障报告，并采集本地崩溃转储与内存快照以便排查。 - **插件来源与 `Computer Use (Next)`:** “来源配置”新增 `dshfind` 插件索引一键填充与切换，“自定义插件市场”统一更名为“自定义插件索引”；桌面内置 Computer Use 插件更名为 `Computer Use (Next)`（命令仍为 `/computer-use`）。 - **Agents Anywhere 2.1.0:** 支持在网页端直接接力正在运行的 Codex 桌面会话，查看会话保持只读、仅在发言或操作时短暂接管，实现电脑与手机在同一会话轮流对话；会话顶部切换模型或权限立即生效，Agent 提问改为会话内异步表单；“设置 → 维护”新增“重装依赖环境”，支持切换 PyPI 镜像重建运行环境并自动从官方源补齐缺失包。  ## 修复  - **macOS 重启权限保持:** 修复 macOS 下切换 Profile、恢复重启或插件变更重启后可能丢失系统“本地网络”权限的问题。 - **会话链接容错:** 对话中出现无法解析的 `dsh-session:` 文本时按普通文本保留，不再导致当前轮次对话报错中断。 - **Linux 打包与兼容:** 新增 Linux ARM64 打包校验；Linux AppImage 改用静态运行时，不再依赖系统安装 `libfuse.so.2`；修复 Next 打包时的社区市场依赖解析问题及上游 ACP 模式诊断输出混入 `stdout` 的问题。 - **Agents Anywhere 同步稳定性:** 修复接入已有 Codex 会话后历史消息乱序或交错的问题（升级后下次同步自动校准），消除搜索、图片查看与上下文压缩产生的重复记录，并修复多插件连接同一 Codex 时的交接超时与异常序号写入阻塞问题。  ## 下载  从本页 **Assets** 下载对应平台的安装包：  | 平台 | 文件 | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  可使用本页 `SHA256SUMS.txt` 核验全部安装包。  ## 使用提示  - 请先退出旧版应用并备份 `~/.dsh`（或在“设置 → 桌面设置”中使用“备份”），安装后验证常用插件和工作流程。 - 如需通过应用内终端管理 `desktop` Profile 的插件，请先启动 Desktop 完成该 Profile 的初始化。 - 从 2.0.17-next 或更早版本升级时，建议手动下载安装；旧版应用内更新可能无法完成下载。 - Office 文档技能首次使用时将随包运行时安装到 `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime`，需要额外磁盘空间。 - 默认与旧稳定版共用 `~/.dsh`；Profile 不隔离同一目录中的会话、账号和凭据。 - 可在“设置 → 桌面设置”或恢复助手中切换数据目录，也可通过绝对路径 `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` 隔离 DSH 数据（应用内选择优先于环境变量），但不会因此隔离 AA 的默认共享登录与设备绑定状态。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.20-next...v2.0.21-next) · [项目网站](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT 已取代原稳定版通道。  ---  # English  This Next release upgrades upstream to DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.4, improves launch speed and window state restoration, upgrades Profile repair, undo, and failure reporting in the recovery assistant, adds `dshfind` plugin index support, and upgrades the bundled Agents Anywhere plugin to 2.1.0 (adding Codex session handoff and dual-device turn-taking, dependency environment reinstallation, and history ordering fixes).  DSH NEXT is a community-maintained open-source project, not an official DeepSeek product.  **Upstream version:** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.1-rc.4](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.1-rc.4).  ## New and improved  - **Startup speed and window experience:** Sped up macOS proxy detection when no system proxy is set and parallelized login-shell environment loading, enabled frontend script compilation caching for faster warm launches, and persisted the main window’s position, size, and maximized state across restarts. - **Recovery assistant and Profile repair:** "Back up and repair current Profile" now creates a full backup before resetting the Profile to default template plugins and re-running first-launch onboarding, with a new "Undo last repair" action on Quick Recovery for one-click restoration. Opening the recovery assistant automatically generates a single-file failure report and captures local crash dumps and memory snapshots for troubleshooting. - **Plugin sources and `Computer Use (Next)`:** Added one-click `dshfind` plugin index configuration and switching in Source Configuration, renamed "Custom plugin markets" to "Custom plugin indexes", and renamed the bundled desktop Computer Use plugin to `Computer Use (Next)` (keeping the `/computer-use` command). - **Agents Anywhere 2.1.0:** Continue running Codex desktop sessions directly from the web while keeping viewing read-only and taking over briefly only when sending messages or performing actions so desktop and mobile can take turns in the same session; model and permission changes in the session header take effect immediately, and agent questions now use in-session async forms; added "Reinstall dependency environment" under **Settings → Maintenance** with PyPI mirror switching and automatic fallback to official PyPI for missing packages.  ## Fixes  - **macOS permission preservation on restart:** Fixed in-app restarts (Profile switches, recovery restarts, and plugin changes) potentially losing macOS Local Network permissions. - **Session link resilience:** Preserved undecodable `dsh-session:` text in conversations as plain text instead of failing the turn. - **Linux packaging and compatibility:** Added Linux ARM64 packaging verification; switched Linux AppImage to a static runtime so `libfuse.so.2` is no longer required on the host system; fixed community market peer dependency resolution during Next packaging and upstream ACP diagnostics leaking into `stdout`. - **Agents Anywhere sync stability:** Fixed out-of-order or interleaved history after attaching to an existing Codex session (automatically recalibrated on the next sync after upgrading), eliminated duplicate timeline entries from searches, image views, and context compaction, and fixed multi-plugin Codex handoff timeouts and oversized sequence number write stalls.  ## Downloads  Choose the matching installer from **Assets** below:  | Platform | File | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.21-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  Use `SHA256SUMS.txt` from this release to verify all installers.  ## Usage notes  - Quit the previous version and back up `~/.dsh` (or use **Back up** in **Settings → Desktop settings**) before installing. Verify your usual plugins and workflows afterward. - Before managing plugins for the `desktop` Profile from the bundled terminal, launch Desktop to initialize that Profile. - When upgrading from 2.0.17-next or earlier, download and install manually; in-app updates in those versions may not complete the download. - The Office skills install the bundled runtime under `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime` on first use, requiring additional disk space. - Defaults to the shared `~/.dsh` home. Profiles do not isolate sessions, accounts, or credentials within that home. - Change the data directory in **Settings → Desktop settings** or the recovery assistant, or set `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` to an absolute path to isolate DSH data (an in-app choice takes precedence over the environment variable); this does not isolate AA’s default shared sign-in and device-binding state.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.20-next...v2.0.21-next) · [Project website](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT replaces the former Stable edition channel.

### 2026-11-01T03:37:37Z [**ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI**](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI)

```
自 v0.17.0 以来，dsh-TUI 跟上 DSH `0.2.1-rc.5` 验证线，修好升级 DSH 后 TUI 被拒绝加载的问题， 让首次启动引导可以直接登录或加 key，接入官方 Git 工作树实验包，并让插件授权跟随插件的安装、启用与卸载。  ## 亮点  - **适配 DSH `0.2.1-rc.5`，`0.2.1-rc.4` 列为兼容线** — v0.17.0 只声明支持到 `0.2.1-rc.3`；npm 上的 DSH 升到 rc.4 / rc.5 后，DSH 的插件兼容检查会拒绝加载它，表现为升级 DSH 后 TUI 打不开。本版把 rc.4 / rc.5 加入验证线与 peer 范围，`dst` 自动化适配器的验证版本同步加入。按发布包逐文件比对，TUI 用到的上游代码在 rc.3 到 rc.5 之间只改了版本号，宿主复刻指纹不变。([#1806](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1806) by @AdamPlatin123) - **首次启动引导可以直接登录或加 key** — 引导第一步现在能登录订阅账号、添加模型提供方（API key）、重新检查或跳过这一步；订阅登录带回的新模型按 0/1/N 处理：只报结果、直接设为默认，或在内联选择器里挑一个。「添加模型提供方」沿用 `/provider` 向导，向导的切换题会逐项列出本次启用的模型，并保留「保持当前模型」。([#1647](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1647) by @AdamPlatin123) - **官方 Git 工作树实验包** — `/plugin list` 里可以直接开启 DSH 官方的 `create_worktree`；工具卡标题显示分支名，结果用两行写明检出位置和起点。Minimal 与随包的 liangshen 预设不提供这个工具：Minimal 的持久 shell 不跟随目录，liangshen 为跨平台统一规则一并隐藏。工作树不会自动清理，分支需要另外删除。([#1745](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1745) by @MomentDerek) - **插件授权跟随插件生命周期** — 安装或启用时按包内声明授予，关闭时撤销，卸载时删除该组件的授权条目，复用同一组件 id 的新包不会静默继承旧授权；确认清单按终端高度折叠，放不下时显示剩余条数。([#1747](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1747) by @Lucas2011wastaken)  ## 需要留意的行为变化  - **`/plugin enable` 一并授予声明的权限** — 确认页逐行列出包内声明的权限，确认后启用并按声明授予，写入的范围不会超出声明；不想授予就按 `Esc` 放弃。([#1747](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1747) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **`@~/…` 在任意深度都指向家目录** — 与 shell 一致；工作区里真有一个叫 `~` 的目录时，改写成 `@./~/…` 引用。裸 `@~` 的行为不变。([#1739](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1739) by @AdamPlatin123)  ## 修复  - **会话**：DSH 内核上 dsh-tui 行随 `agents` 服务卸载时，屏幕不再停在「回合进行中」，而是显示会话已结束；没送达的输入暂存、不自动重发，行恢复后重新挂接并重开会话，`/restart` 会带回排队的文字。([#1759](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1759) by @MomentDerek) - **输入**：`@~/` 补全选中子目录后不再消失，发送的 `@~/…` 不再落进 missing；侧栏聚焦时 `Ctrl+V` / `Alt+V` 粘贴进 btw 输入框而不是主聊天草稿，全屏 btw 也能粘贴。([#1739](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1739) by @AdamPlatin123；[#1785](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1785) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **面板与渲染**：待办侧栏按宽度完整折行显示长目标、可滚动查看全部条目；展开的工具卡保留用户设置的「工具卡背景」。([#1748](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1748) by @Lucas2011wastaken；[#1782](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1782) by @baobaolaodie) - **宿主与插件**：带额外第三方 bundle 的 profile 启动时，慢 core 行恢复后的官方 Remote service 注册不再被 root guard 误拒（宿主在装 guard 时物化 root-owned `invocation` accessor，guard 判定面不变）；desktop/web profile 下插件 activation 的根事件派发与服务解析也不再被守卫拒绝，设置保存、模型增删与插件卸载恢复可用。结构性的 root 操作仍被拒绝。([#1732](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1732) by @Lucas2011wastaken；[#1804](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1804) by @MomentDerek)  ## 兼容与升级  - **DSH 主适配线**：`0.2.1-rc.5`；`0.2.1-rc.4`、`0.2.1-rc.3`、`0.2.1-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.1`、`0.2.0-rc.2` 以及更早的验证线仍在契约内，契约外版本打漂移警告。 - **升级 DSH 后 v0.17.0 打不开的用户**：TUI 进不去时用不了程序内的 `/update`，请直接运行下面的 npm 命令升级到 v0.18.0。曾用 `dsh plugin allow-version` 给 v0.17.0 加过豁免的，升级后不再需要。 - **从 v0.17.0 升级**：程序内 `/update`，或   `npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.18.0`。 - **Windows 离线安装器**：DSH 验证范围随契约更新到 `0.2.1-rc.5`。已装 rc.5 直接保留，rc.4 升到 rc.5，不再像 v0.17.0 的安装器那样降回 rc.3。  ## 安装  sh npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.18.0   偏好一键安装的用户可下载 Release 中的 `dsh-tui-setup.zip`（解压后 macOS / Linux 运行 `sh install.sh`，Windows 双击 `install.bat`） 或 Windows 离线安装程序 `dsh-tui-setup-win-x64.exe`；便携包解压即用。   <!-- dsh-tui:generated-notes --> <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.18.0 -->  ## What's Changed * feat(plugin): adopt the official Git worktree experimental plugin by @MomentDerek in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1745 * fix(panels): wrap long goals and scroll the todo side panel by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1748 * feat(plugin): follow grants through the plugin lifecycle by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1747 * fix(mentions): expand ~/ and ~\ for @ completion and attachments by @AdamPlatin123 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1739 * fix(channel): settle and hold a DSH session whose listener context unloads (#1651) by @MomentDerek in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1759 * chore(deps): adapt the validated line to DSH 0.2.1-rc.5 by @AdamPlatin123 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1806 * fix(btw): route paste to the panel column instead of the main draft by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1785 * fix(tool-card): keep the configured background when expanded by @baobaolaodie in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1782 * feat(onboarding): sign in or add a key in the first step, then adopt models 0/1/N by @AdamPlatin123 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1647 * fix(host): materialize the root-owned invocation accessor with the guard (#1616) by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1732 * fix(dsh-adapter): keep root event dispatch and registry reads open to plugins by @MomentDerek in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1804  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/compare/v0.17.0...v0.18.0

# SubBoost v2.11.0  ## 中文  - 扩展自定义规则，支持更多 Mihomo 规则类型、AND/OR/NOT 逻辑组合及批量导入格式，改进规则参数和目标的保留。 - 新增订阅链接重置，可在订阅设置中更换链接并使旧链接失效。 - 改进规则格式识别、兼容健康检查的共享，以及移动端设置与弹窗布局。 - 改进部分 VLESS Reality 链接的导入兼容性。 - 修复大备份校验误报导致自部署升级中止的问题。  升级提示：如果旧版更新时出现 `read=141 pg_restore=0`，请先使用本次发布附带的独立修复脚本更新管理器，再运行 `subboost update`。修复脚本保留备份校验，不修改数据库和应用配置。  ## English  - Expand custom rules with more Mihomo rule types, AND/OR/NOT logical combinations, and batch import formats, with improved preservation of rule parameters and targets. - Add subscription link reset in subscription settings, replacing the link and invalidating the previous one. - Improve rule format detection, sharing of compatible health checks, and mobile settings and dialog layouts. - Improve import compatibility for certain VLESS Reality links. - Fix false verification failures on large backups that could abort self-hosted updates.  Upgrade note: If an older installation reports `read=141 pg_restore=0` during an update, first use this release's standalone repair script to refresh the manager, then run `subboost update`. The repair script preserves backup verification and does not modify the database or application configuration. 
```

### 2026-10-28T19:46:53Z [**Gloridust/WechatOnCloud**](https://github.com/Gloridust/WechatOnCloud)

```
## ⬆️ 升级方式（重要）  1. 面板「检查更新」把面板升到 1.6.0（在 `.env` 里固定了 `WOC_VERSION` 的，改成 `1.6.0` 后执行 `docker compose pull && docker compose up -d`）； 2. **再点「一键升级全部实例」**：KasmVNC 升级、大图粘贴卡死的根治都在实例镜像里，**只更新面板不会生效**。  升级实例会保留全部数据（登录状态、聊天记录、设备标识）。  ---  ## ⚠️ 行为变化：同一实例同一时间只在一处显示  以前多台设备 / 多个标签页可以同时看同一个实例（一人操作，其余只读）。现在同一时间只在一处显示：在另一台设备或另一个标签页打开，就直接接管过来；原来的页面提示「已在别处打开」，点「在这里继续使用」可以接回。两边不会自动互相抢。  为什么：同一实例有多条桌面连接时，KasmVNC 会泄漏内存（两条连接、画面滚动 1 分钟就涨约 160MB，kasmtech/KasmVNC#371，官方修复还没发版）；以前「桌面卡死、要重启实例才能连上」也和多条连接并存有关。  ---  ## ✨ 远程桌面：KasmVNC 升级到 1.5.2  实例里的 KasmVNC 原来停在 2024 年底的版本（上游基础镜像已经不再更新），这次换成 Kasm 官方发布的 1.5.2：  - 断线后偶发弹出「Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'lastActiveAt')」、只能整页刷新——上游已修复； - 网络抖一下、非正常断开后不会自己重连——现在会自动连回； - 画面编码更省 CPU。  顺带修了面板这边一个问题：页面关掉或设备断网后，面板到实例的那段连接可能一直留着，KasmVNC 以为还有人在看（「幽灵会话」）。现在浏览器这边一断，两段一起断。  无感输入、底部输入条、粘贴、文件传输、声音都在新版上回归测试过。  ---  ## 🐛 修复  ### 大于 1MB 的图片粘贴卡死应用；QQ 里 Ctrl+V 黑屏（#165、#159） KasmVNC 去读超过约 1MB 的 PNG 剪贴板时永远读不完，剪贴板就此卡住，应用一读剪贴板就挂住（QQ 表现为黑屏，要重启 QQ 才恢复）。升级实例后从根上不再卡，截图按原图粘贴；只有反代限制了上传大小等情况下才自动压缩后重试。另外，按住粘贴键稍久不会再一次粘出两三张图。  代价：在应用里复制的图片，不再自动同步到本机剪贴板（https 访问时原来会）。本机图片粘进应用不受影响，文字剪贴板同步照旧。  ### 无感输入上屏变慢（#155） 1.5.1 起每段中文、每次回车 / 退格都多等约 160ms，现在回到 1.5.0 的速度。  ### 创建实例失败时什么都看不出来（#129） 创建改为后台进行，卡片上显示「创建中」和镜像拉取进度；失败时卡片和实例日志写明原因和怎么办，可以一键「重试创建」。拉取镜像卡住 2 分钟即判定失败，不再无限挂着；面板启动也不会再被拉镜像拖住几分钟。  ### 公众号文章 / 内置浏览器崩溃（SIGILL）的诊断（#166） 本版**没有修复**这个崩溃。诊断包新增宿主 CPU 指令集、实例实际运行参数和崩溃转储摘要；遇到的话请导出诊断包附在 issue 里，就能判断是哪种崩溃。  ---  ## ↩️ 回滚  出现问题时可回到 1.5.2：在 `.env` 里设 `WOC_VERSION=1.5.2`，执行 `docker compose up -d`，再在面板里「一键升级全部实例」（实例切回 1.5.2 镜像，数据保留）。  - **回滚前先删掉「创建失败」的实例卡片**：1.5.2 的面板不认识这个标记，启动时会为这些没有容器的实例同步重新拉镜像，面板几分钟打不开。 - 回到 1.5.2 后，多端可以同时观看；大于 1MB 的图片粘贴卡死的问题也会回来。  镜像：`ghcr.io/gloridust/{woc-panel,wechat-on-cloud}:1.6.0`、`gloridust/{woc-panel,wechat-on-cloud}:1.6.0`（amd64 + arm64）。 
```

### 2026-11-01T07:02:08Z [**AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm**](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm)

```
[中文](#中文) | [English](#english)  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.3.0 的首要更新是终端内存改造：重新设计历史存储，减少搜索临时分配，并回收后台标签页和图片的闲置缓存。这一版还扩展了 Kitty 与 Sixel 图形支持，加入 BLE 蓝牙终端，并改进大文件编辑、Markdown 预览和 SFTP 文件操作。  #### ⚡ 终端内存大幅降低  ##### 两万行满行历史，从 196.47 MB 降到 15.50 MB  ![20,000 行、400 列历史内存对比：普通 ASCII 从 196.47 MB 降至 15.50 MB，每行十段着色从 196.47 MB 降至 21.26 MB](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/v2.3.0/.github/release-notes/assets/terminal-memory-2.3.0-comparison.png)  以前，满行历史中的每个字符都要保存一份完整颜色和属性。现在，普通历史行把文本与样式分开保存，相邻文字共享样式；即使每个字符都不同、整行都写满，也能省下大量内存。  在 **20,000 行历史 × 400 列，另含 40 行屏幕**的相同工作负载中：  | 满行文本场景 | 完整单元格基线 | 本版紧凑历史 | 降幅 | | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | | 普通 ASCII 文本 | 196.47 MB | **15.50 MB** | **92.1%** | | 每行十段着色 | 196.47 MB | **21.26 MB** | **89.2%** |  数据来自本轮分阶段基准，完整单元格基线已包含尾部压缩。测量范围是终端与解析器的堆分配，MB 使用十进制；完整环境和各阶段结果见[终端基准](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/docs/development/performance-and-benchmarking.md#text-run-history-and-sequential-reads-2026-10-26)。  重复尾部继续压缩；样式变化频繁的行按存储成本选择 8 字节紧凑单元格或完整单元格。宽字符、组合字符、超链接和颜色信息保持完整。搜索和复制直接读取紧凑历史，避免为了读取而把整份历史展开。  ##### 搜索时少复制，多开终端时及时回收  - 搜索共享文本快照，并压缩复杂 Unicode 和跨行匹配的位置记录。在中文与组合字符的正则命中基准中，位置映射优化将额外峰值堆占用从 **119.79 MiB 降至 74.13 MiB**。 - 隐藏的终端标签页释放字形排版、绘制、高亮和链接布局缓存，重新显示时再构建。会话、选区、滚动位置和搜索状态继续保留。在 8 个终端、其中 7 个隐藏的基准中，额外堆分配从 **60.02 MiB 降至 35.25 MiB**。 - 静态终端图片共享像素，多个放置不复制整张图片。图片淘汰时同步释放后台持有的数据，隐藏终端释放派生渲染缓存；动图继续复用完整帧，避免用反复解码换取更低的缓存占用。  #### ✨ 图形与设备新功能  ##### Kitty 与 Sixel：图片跟随文本，动画复用完整帧  - Kitty 加入 Unicode 占位符、虚拟放置和相对定位。图片可以跟随占位文本或父图片移动，主屏与备用屏切换、局部滚动、窗口重排和历史裁剪也会同步更新图片位置。 - Kitty 动画支持帧合成、局部更新、透明混合、循环次数、暂停与继续、加载等待和帧删除。播放复用完整解码帧，到下一帧实际需要显示时才刷新。 - Sixel 图片随正文滚动、重排和清理。文字覆盖或擦除时，只清除对应的图片区域；插入、删除字符时，相应图片部分跟着移动。tmux 分屏中的图片保持原始尺寸，并裁剪在所属面板内。 - 修复 Retina 等高分屏上图片被放大、裁掉一部分或像素偏移错误的问题；Kitty 图片到达屏幕底部时也不再盖住后续提示符。  ##### 新增 BLE 蓝牙终端  授予系统蓝牙权限后，可以扫描适配器和附近设备、查看 GATT 服务，再连接 Nordic UART Service 或具有兼容通知／写入特征的自定义服务。（PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731)）  - 支持文本和十六进制收发，可分别设置发送、接收换行，并配置本地回显、MTU 和写入方式。连接可以保存、编辑和重连，也支持云同步与加密 `.oxide` 导入导出。（PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731)） - 新建连接时可以选择分组，已有 BLE 配置也能在会话管理器中移动分组。（PR [#735](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/735)） - 改善设备身份匹配，同名设备无法区分时会给出提示。取消扫描或连接后，过时的结果不会再打开会话；关闭连接会清理通知订阅和设备连接。（PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731)）  #### 🛠️ 功能详情与修复  ##### 终端交互修复  - 本地终端在后台回复光标、颜色和窗口尺寸查询，不再等待界面处理，修复程序等待响应以及响应内容混入 Shell 输入的问题。SSH 终端也能回复像素尺寸查询。（Issue [#733](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/733)） - 串口文本模式会正确解析 Shell 标记、工作目录等有效 OSC 序列，不再把它们显示成普通文字；异常启动输出的处理仍然保留。（Issue [#732](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/732)）  ##### 大文件编辑与 Markdown 阅读  - 编辑、撤销、保存和语法分析共享分块文本，减少整份文件的复制。批量编辑一次更新文本结构，删除大片内容时复用原有存储来保存撤销信息。 - 搜索在后台执行，文本扫描不再占用界面线程，匹配范围也使用更紧凑的存储。百万匹配专项基准中的额外堆占用从 **16.00 MiB 降至 4.78 MiB**。 - 超长行增加水平可见区的分段排版，折行和折叠使用紧凑行映射。后台编辑器释放排版、高亮和语法树缓存，未保存正文、撤销历史与滚动位置继续保留。 - IDE 中的 `.md` 文件默认进入预览，可一键切换到源码。预览支持鼠标选择、跨段落复制和全选，复制得到阅读文本。切回编辑时保留焦点、未保存内容和撤销记录。（PR [#726](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/726)） - Markdown 预览共享解析结果，用高度索引定位可见内容，减少滚动长文档时的重复遍历。IDE、本地文件和 SFTP 预览都会复用未变化内容的解析结果，在切换模式或关闭后释放不再使用的缓存。  10,000 段 Markdown 的额外布局内存从 **6.26 MiB 降至 0.31 MiB**，测量不含原始正文、解析树、字形和图片。编辑器与 Markdown 的对比条件见[编辑器内存记录](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/docs/development/native-editor-memory.md)。  ##### 图片传输与资源限制  - Kitty 支持分块上传、压缩载荷、图片编号与多处放置，以及裁剪、像素偏移、层级和多种删除条件。同一图片的多个放置共享数据。 - Kitty 文件与共享内存传输可在会话授权后直接访问本地资源，无需使用私有传输目录。可从终端菜单提前授权，也可在请求被阻止后授权并重试。**该权限也适用于会话中的远端程序**，关闭或重连后失效；直接传输图片数据不需要这项权限。 - Sixel 解码先检查完整画布和内存预算，再一次分配所需空间。支持像素比例、颜色寄存器、透明背景、能力与尺寸探测，以及固定显示和随文本滚动两种模式。 - 默认质量档的每终端图片保留预算提高到 **256 MiB**，随图片传入按需占用。终端图片、解码与上传缓冲、背景图片共用 **512 MiB CPU 图片预算**，GPU 存储另计。单帧和像素数量限制仍然生效，低功耗与兼容档保留较小预算。  ##### 图片、知识库与后台内存  - 图片按显示尺寸解码，放大或移到高分屏后再加载更清晰的版本。PNG、JPEG、背景图和旋转预览的解码路径都做了调整，降低首次打开大图片的内存峰值。 - 默认图片缓存按字节占用清退闲置内容，关闭 SFTP 预览后释放相应资源。CPU 像素与上传数据减少重复副本，空闲界面内存池也会回收多余容量。 - 动图加载时解码并缓存完整帧，播放时直接复用；可见动画复用已上传帧，隐藏后停止无效刷新并释放相应纹理。 - 聊天历史、知识库和传输进度数据库改用较小的页缓存预算。知识库切到后台后释放可重建缓存，统计信息不再每次绘制都扫描向量。向量索引构建减少数据复制，保存和加载改为流式处理，并限制解压大小。 - WSL 图形桌面减少一份完整 CPU 帧副本，降低高分辨率桌面的驻留内存。  ##### SFTP 与会话管理  - SFTP 侧边栏可以切换列表或目录树，按需展开子目录，并记住视图偏好。目录树会处理符号链接循环，右键操作使用选中条目的完整路径。（PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)） - 可以把 SFTP 选中目录同步到对应终端，终端忙碌时会提示。将文件拖入 SSH 终端，可通过对应连接上传到该终端的当前目录。（PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)） - 路径书签保存一组本地与远端目录，支持重命名、删除和按连接管理；远端到远端标签页不提供路径书签。（PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)） - 上传和文件选择流程会检查目标连接、目录是否已经变化，防止切换页面后仍向旧目标上传。（PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)） - 活动 SSH 会话按主机和用户分组，显示已保存连接的名称，并区分端口和跳板路径，方便辨认同一主机上的不同会话。（PR [#727](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/727)）  ##### SSH、云同步与配置  - SSH 协议依赖升级到 russh 0.65.0，保留 OxideTerm 的既有补丁，并更新依赖锁和 Nix 源码配置。 - Windows 启动 SSH `ProxyCommand` 辅助程序时，不再弹出额外控制台窗口。（PR [#730](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/730)） - 修复阿里云 OSS 的 S3 兼容寻址，支持官方 S3 端点、原生 OSS 端点及已包含同一存储桶名称的端点。使用说明补充区域、权限和 STS Session Token 配置。（PR [#725](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/725)） - 同步独立连接配置时，预览和实际传输都会遵循用户选择的删除记录，覆盖上传、下载及 `.oxide` 导入导出。（PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731)）  ##### 窗口与日常交互  - 修复工作区启动焦点，统一主窗口和独立窗口的关闭、退出行为。macOS 全屏隐藏失败后可以恢复，锁定应用时退出会给出明确提示。（PR [#721](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/721)） - 命令面板优先显示名称相关的结果，减少描述文字造成的误命中，并修正搜索来源和文本高亮。（PR [#722](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/722)） - 复选框的动画状态相互独立，切换一项不再让另一项的对号消失。 - 终端滚动条及点击范围统一为 8 px。仪表盘卡片与标题区域对齐，移除没有实际限制作用的固定“容量 128”显示。  #### 👥 贡献者  - @BilityLeu：工作区焦点、窗口关闭和命令面板搜索改进（PR [#721](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/721)、[#722](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/722)）。 - @livy：Markdown 默认预览和阿里云 OSS 兼容修复（PR [#725](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/725)、[#726](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/726)）。 - @idxdy：活动会话分组、SFTP 目录与传输工作流，以及 Windows `ProxyCommand` 窗口修复（PR [#727](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/727)、[#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)、[#730](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/730)）。 - @allegrox：BLE 蓝牙终端连接及 BLE 会话分组支持（PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731)、[#735](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/735)）。  本版更新范围：[v2.2.6…v2.3.0](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.6...v2.3.0)。  ### English  Terminal memory is the main change in OxideTerm 2.3.0: redesigned history storage, fewer temporary search allocations, and reclamation of idle caches in background tabs and images. This release also expands Kitty and Sixel graphics support, adds BLE terminals, and improves large-file editing, Markdown previews, and SFTP workflows.  #### ⚡ Substantially lower terminal memory use  ##### 20,000 full history rows: from 196.47 MB to 15.50 MB  ![Memory for 20,000 history rows at 400 columns: full ASCII falls from 196.47 MB to 15.50 MB; ten color runs per line fall from 196.47 MB to 21.26 MB](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/v2.3.0/.github/release-notes/assets/terminal-memory-2.3.0-comparison.png)  Previously, each character in a full history row stored a full copy of its colors and attributes. Ordinary history rows now store text separately from styles, letting adjacent text share them. This saves memory even when every character is different and every column is filled.  With the same workload of **20,000 history rows × 400 columns, plus 40 screen rows**:  | Full-line text workload | Full-cell baseline | Compact history in this release | Reduction | | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | | Ordinary ASCII text | 196.47 MB | **15.50 MB** | **92.1%** | | Ten color runs per line | 196.47 MB | **21.26 MB** | **89.2%** |  These figures come from the staged benchmarks for this work; the full-cell baseline already included tail compression. They measure terminal/parser heap allocations in decimal MB. The [terminal benchmark record](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/docs/development/performance-and-benchmarking.md#text-run-history-and-sequential-reads-2026-10-26) documents the environment and each stage.  Repeated tails remain compressed. Rows with frequent style changes use eight-byte compact cells or full cells according to their storage cost. Wide characters, combining marks, hyperlinks, and colors are preserved. Search and copy read compact history directly instead of expanding the entire history to access its contents.  ##### Fewer search copies and smaller background-tab caches  - Search shares text snapshots and uses smaller position records for complex Unicode and cross-line matches. In the CJK-and-combining-character regex-hit benchmark, the position-mapping optimization reduced extra peak heap from **119.79 MiB to 74.13 MiB**. - Hidden terminal tabs release glyph, paint, highlighting, and link-layout caches, rebuilding them when shown. Sessions, selections, scroll positions, and search state remain intact. In the eight-terminal benchmark with seven tabs hidden, additional heap allocations fell from **60.02 MiB to 35.25 MiB**. - Static terminal images share pixels across placements. Eviction releases the image data held by the backend, and hidden terminals release derived rendering caches. Animations continue to reuse complete frames rather than repeatedly decoding them to shrink the cache.  #### ✨ Graphics and device support  ##### Kitty and Sixel: images follow text, animations reuse cached frames  - Kitty now supports Unicode placeholders, virtual placements, and relative positioning. Images can follow placeholder text or a parent image, with placement updates across main/alternate screen switches, partial scrolling, reflow, and history trimming. - Kitty animations support frame composition, partial updates, transparency, loop counts, pause/resume, loading waits, and frame deletion. Playback reuses complete decoded frames and redraws when the next frame is due. - Sixel images follow text through scrolling, reflow, and cleanup. Text overwrite or erasure clears only the affected image area; character insertion and deletion move the corresponding image strips. Images keep their native size inside tmux panes and stay within pane boundaries. - Fixed oversized, clipped, or incorrectly offset images on Retina and other high-DPI displays. Kitty images reaching the bottom of the screen no longer cover subsequent prompts.  ##### New BLE terminal connections  With operating-system Bluetooth permission, you can scan adapters and nearby devices, inspect GATT services, and connect to Nordic UART Service or custom services with compatible notification/write characteristics. (PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731))  - Send and display text or hexadecimal data, set input/output line endings separately, and configure local echo, MTU, and write mode. Connections can be saved, edited, reconnected, cloud-synced, and included in encrypted `.oxide` imports and exports. (PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731)) - Choose a group when creating a BLE connection or move an existing profile between groups in Session Manager. (PR [#735](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/735)) - Improved device identity matching, with a notice when device names are ambiguous. Canceled discovery or connection attempts cannot reopen a session through stale results, and closing a connection cleans up notifications and the device link. (PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731))  #### 🛠️ Changes and fixes  ##### Terminal interaction fixes  - Local terminals answer cursor, color, and window-size queries on the background worker without waiting for the UI, fixing programs waiting for replies and replies appearing as Shell input. SSH terminals also answer pixel-size queries. (Issue [#733](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/733)) - Serial text mode correctly parses valid OSC sequences such as Shell markers and working-directory reports instead of printing them. Handling for malformed startup output remains in place. (Issue [#732](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues/732))  ##### Large-file editing and Markdown reading  - Editing, undo, saving, and syntax analysis share chunked text to avoid full-file copies. Batched edits update the text structure once, and large deletions reuse the existing storage for undo. - Search runs in the background, keeping scans off the UI thread and storing match ranges compactly. Additional heap use in the million-match benchmark fell from **16.00 MiB to 4.78 MiB**. - Added horizontal viewport segmentation for very long lines and compact wrap/fold maps. Background editors release layout, highlighting, and syntax-tree caches while keeping unsaved text, undo history, and scroll position. - IDE `.md` files open in preview, with a one-click switch to source. Preview supports mouse selection, copying across paragraphs, and Select All, producing the rendered reading text. Returning to editing preserves focus, unsaved content, and undo history. (PR [#726](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/726)) - Markdown previews share parsed trees and locate visible content through a height index, reducing repeated traversal when scrolling long documents. IDE, local-file, and SFTP previews reuse parsing for unchanged content and release unused caches after mode changes or close.  Additional layout memory for 10,000 Markdown paragraphs fell from **6.26 MiB to 0.31 MiB**, excluding the original text, parsed tree, glyphs, and images. The [editor memory report](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/docs/development/native-editor-memory.md) documents the editor and Markdown comparison conditions.  ##### Image transfers and resource limits  - Kitty supports chunked uploads, compressed payloads, image identifiers, multiple placements, cropping, pixel offsets, layers, and additional deletion selectors. Placements of the same image share data. - Kitty file and shared-memory transfers can access local resources after per-session authorization, without a private transfer directory. Allow access from the terminal menu beforehand, or grant it after a blocked request and retry. **Remote programs in the session receive this permission too.** It expires on close or reconnect; direct image-data transfers do not need it. - Sixel decoding checks the complete canvas and memory budget before allocating the required space once. It supports pixel aspect ratios, color registers, transparency, capability and geometry queries, and both fixed-display and text-scrolling modes. - The default quality profile's per-terminal image retention budget is now **256 MiB**, used as images arrive. Terminal images, decode/upload buffers, and backgrounds share a **512 MiB CPU image budget**; GPU storage is separate. Per-frame and pixel-count limits still apply, with smaller budgets in low-power and compatibility profiles.  ##### Images, knowledge, and background memory  - Images decode at their displayed size and load sharper versions after zooming or moving to a higher-DPI display. Updated PNG, JPEG, background-image, and rotated-preview decoding to reduce peak memory when opening large images. - The default image cache reclaims idle entries by byte usage, and closing an SFTP preview releases its resources. CPU/upload paths keep fewer duplicate pixel buffers, and idle UI memory pools release excess capacity. - Animated images decode and cache complete frames on load, then reuse them during playback. Visible animations reuse uploaded frames; hidden views stop unnecessary redraws and release their textures. - Chat history, knowledge storage, and transfer progress use smaller database page-cache budgets. Background knowledge views release rebuildable caches, and statistics no longer scan vectors on every render. Vector-index construction makes fewer copies; saving and loading use streaming I/O with bounded decompression. - WSL graphical desktops keep one fewer full CPU-frame copy, reducing resident memory at high resolutions.  ##### SFTP and session management  - The SFTP sidebar switches between list and tree views, loads subdirectories on demand, and remembers the view preference. The tree handles symbolic-link cycles, and context-menu actions use the selected entry's full path. (PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)) - Send the selected SFTP directory to its terminal, with a notice if the terminal is busy. Files dropped onto an SSH terminal can upload to its current directory through the corresponding connection. (PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)) - Path bookmarks save paired local/remote directories, with rename, delete, and per-connection organization. Remote-to-remote tabs do not offer path bookmarks. (PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)) - Upload and file-picker workflows check for changes to the target connection or directory, preventing uploads to an old destination after navigation. (PR [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729)) - Active SSH sessions are grouped by host and user, show saved connection names, and distinguish ports and jump-host routes so sessions on the same host are easier to identify. (PR [#727](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/727))  ##### SSH, cloud sync, and configuration  - Upgraded the SSH protocol dependency to russh 0.65.0, keeping OxideTerm's existing patches and updating the dependency lock and Nix source configuration. - SSH `ProxyCommand` helpers no longer open an extra console window on Windows. (PR [#730](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/730)) - Fixed Alibaba Cloud OSS addressing through its S3-compatible API, supporting official S3 endpoints, native OSS endpoints, and endpoints already qualified with the same bucket name. Setup guidance now covers regions, permissions, and STS Session Tokens. (PR [#725](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/725)) - Standalone-profile sync respects selected deletion records in both previews and transfers, including upload, download, and `.oxide` import/export. (PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731))  ##### Window behavior and everyday interactions  - Fixed startup focus and unified closing and exit behavior for main and detached windows. macOS fullscreen hiding can recover from failure, and attempting to quit a locked application shows a clear notice. (PR [#721](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/721)) - Command-palette search prioritizes names, reduces irrelevant matches from descriptions, and preserves search sources and correct text highlighting. (PR [#722](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/722)) - Checkbox animations are independent, so toggling one no longer removes another control's checkmark. - The terminal scrollbar and its hit area are both 8 px wide. Dashboard cards align with the header, and the fixed “capacity 128” readout has been removed because it did not represent an enforced limit.  #### 👥 Contributors  - @BilityLeu: Workspace focus, window closing, and command-palette search improvements (PR [#721](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/721), [#722](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/722)). - @livy: Default Markdown preview and Alibaba Cloud OSS compatibility (PR [#725](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/725), [#726](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/726)). - @idxdy: Active-session grouping, SFTP directory and transfer workflows, and the Windows `ProxyCommand` console fix (PR [#727](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/727), [#729](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/729), [#730](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/730)). - @allegrox: BLE terminal connections and BLE profile grouping (PR [#731](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/731), [#735](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/735)).  Full change range: [v2.2.6…v2.3.0](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/compare/v2.2.6...v2.3.0).  ## 📥 Download for your system  | Operating system | x64 | ARM64 | |---|---|---| | **Windows** | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_windows_x64-setup.exe) | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_windows_arm64-setup.exe) | | **macOS** | [DMG (Intel)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_macos_x64.dmg) | [DMG (Apple Silicon)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_macos_arm64.dmg) | | **Linux** | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_linux_x64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_linux_x64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_linux_x64.rpm) | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_linux_arm64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_linux_arm64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.0/OxideTerm_2.3.0_linux_arm64.rpm) |  Portable archives, signatures, and `sha256sums.txt` remain available in the release assets below.  <details> <summary>📌 Installation Tips / 安装提示</summary>  ### macOS  Downloaded `.dmg` files may be quarantined by Gatekeeper. Run in Terminal:  bash xattr -cr ~/Downloads/OxideTerm_*.dmg # or after install / 或安装后 xattr -cr /Applications/OxideTerm.app   ### Windows  If SmartScreen warns, click **More info** -> **Run anyway**.  若 SmartScreen 弹出警告，点击 **更多信息** -> **仍要运行**。  ### Linux  bash # AppImage chmod +x OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage && ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage  # Debian/Ubuntu sudo dpkg -i OxideTerm_*_linux_*.deb && sudo apt-get install -f  # Fedora/RHEL-compatible systems sudo dnf install ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.rpm   </details>  ## 🔗 Links  - Documentation: https://oxideterm.app - GitHub Issues: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues - Changelog: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/.github/release-notes/stable-changelog.md

### Patch Changes  - [#1194](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1194) [`b645f47`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/b645f47de3e17ed3bf60586c9bebb8092e0f79fd) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `ask-matt` now reads a skill's `SKILL.md` before stating what it does or recommending a step be skipped, instead of trusting its own one-line summaries ([#614](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/614)).  - [#1304](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1304) [`5beb35b`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/5beb35b040e3de97e54fa9e386d7e42d15c295c2) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `chief-of-staff` now keeps its memory in an LLM wiki (an `index.md` plus one page per topic, maintained through ingest, query and lint) and writes it as a historical record: every claim is dated, and every claim about code cites the commit it was true at.  - [#1208](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1208) [`3da8c01`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/3da8c01116264675eeefbd6e9d59c734de9095cc) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `code-review` now searches the repo for standards files and always hands `CODING_STANDARDS.md` / `CONTRIBUTING.md` to the Standards sub-agent ([#1065](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1065)), runs both sub-agents in the foreground and uses their returned reports ([#1073](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1073)), and resolves the issue tracker through the provided tracker doc instead of a hard-coded path ([#937](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/937)).  - [#1209](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1209) [`f3fc563`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/f3fc5632f401156837ee3872f14fe33ccf1024ea) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `diagnosing-bugs` Phase 5 now has the agent `diff` a forced mutation against a pristine copy before trusting the red, so an edit that silently changed nothing can't pass as a failing test ([#955](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/955)).  - [#1187](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1187) [`e0efb6e`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/e0efb6e7435ebb420d8a25d6816ed1ebaa2d23d2) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - Fixed the GitHub tracker template's external-PR listing, which failed on `authorAssociation` ([#468](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/468), thanks @lofi-coding). Re-run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` to refresh `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.  - [#1169](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1169) [`59db609`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/59db609eb1e18aaf59b63284d0e52255aff0c63c) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - The GitHub issue tracker template that `setup-matt-pocock-skills` writes now tells agents to file issues only into the user's own repo, never into `mattpocock/skills`, and to check the target repo before the first `gh issue create`. Agents working in a clone or fork of this repo had filed their specs and tickets here ([#541](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/541) to [#550](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/550)).  - [#1193](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1193) [`95249b0`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/95249b0b49782349740fd9b8c6ce32b4e59e497a) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `grilling` words each question so "yes" accepts the recommended answer, so agreeing never means answering "no" ([#706](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/706)).  - [#1184](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1184) [`4f4e943`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/4f4e9433bbb7c4957ef33b1ea26686fb7c2a4cf4) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `handoff` now names where the OS temp directory is (`$TMPDIR`, else `/tmp`; `%TEMP%` on Windows), so agents stop guessing ([#272](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/272)).  - [#1189](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1189) [`e48341a`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/e48341a3d412cfeba4b9a79d3fd0331fc376ba82) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `implement` now calls the Skill tool for `tdd` and `code-review` instead of bare `/skill` prose, matching [#878](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/878).  - [#1196](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1196) [`6d6a5b9`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/6d6a5b9072f47ee7ba1d972d8ce0ee98b7ed4394) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `implement` fetches a passed ticket reference from the issue tracker and states its title before starting, asking when the reference is ambiguous ([#700](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/700)).  - [#1228](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1228) [`ed0de59`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/ed0de59eb5de8c2730a650cc69f588ffa64c1040) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `setup-matt-pocock-skills` now wires up a git remote before writing a GitHub/GitLab tracker that nothing can reach ([#1201](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1201)), and offers to `git mv` a legacy `CONTEXT.md` / `CONTEXT-MAP.md` to its `GLOSSARY` name ([#1176](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1176)). The GitHub template comments before closing ([#873](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/873)), reads a PR's body as well as its comments ([#1138](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1138)), moves **Blocking** into Conventions with the native `gh` flags ([#855](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/855)), and names the command that counts open blockers ([#1118](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1118)). The GitLab template lists MRs with `-O json` and names the frontier query's command ([#1143](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1143)). Re-run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` to refresh `docs/agents/`.  - [#1197](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1197) [`5b7cade`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/5b7cade4d68947f34dae71326a0ce89f09e22c9c) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `setup-matt-pocock-skills` now creates missing triage labels on GitHub/GitLab ([#616](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/616)), fixes the GitLab template's `glab` commands ([#635](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/635)), reads GitHub issues with `--json` so labels and body come through ([#733](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/733)), and names the "Label in our tracker" column in `triage-labels.md` ([#801](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/801)). - Skills that dispatch subagents (`implement-spec`, `code-review`, `codebase-design`, `improve-codebase-architecture`, `wayfinder`, `research`, `grilling`, `chief-of-staff`) now say to start each one with a fresh context window rather than forking the current conversation, which some harnesses (Codex) do by default and which throws away the clean context a subagent exists for.  - [#1192](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1192) [`3f59913`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/3f599139104dfceabc7754eeb91319802f85e042) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `tdd` now gives each proposed seam a one-line note on what it catches and what it misses, so choosing between seams is no longer a guess ([#607](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/607)).  - [#1183](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1183) [`ad5400b`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/ad5400b930b297b0c0cb231ff1ffe42a7da666aa) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `teach` quizzes vary which position holds the correct answer ([#611](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/611)).  - [#1183](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1183) [`ad5400b`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/ad5400b930b297b0c0cb231ff1ffe42a7da666aa) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `teach` writes its workspace to the directory you ran it in, not the skill folder ([#377](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/377)).  - [#1188](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1188) [`cffab50`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/cffab50350e3d96688f63f890f4d7ca0e6007cfe) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `to-tickets` makes each ticket a sub-issue of its source issue (the GitHub tracker template now gives the command), and omits `## Blocked by` when blockers are native edges. Re-run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` to refresh `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`. Thanks @richardwhatever ([#554](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/554)) and @adamslowe ([#262](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/262)).  - [#1181](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1181) [`8295b8e`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/8295b8e10d4cdb6cce772a56dda7b7c37075074d) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `wayfinder` no longer labels maps or tickets `ready-for-agent` ([#518](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/518)), writes cross-references only with real issue ids ([#507](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/507)), opens no PR from research branches ([#576](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/576)), and resolves each ticket as the type its label names ([#625](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/625)). Thanks @jcwayne, @baob, @rynz and @sponge-b0b.  - [#1237](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1237) [`49dd158`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/49dd158d1076134a641b33efb035946536778336) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `wizard` template fixes: `_existing` decodes a double-quoted `.env` value, so Enter-keeps-current no longer stores the quotes ([#1220](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1220)); stages run inside `run_wizard`, so editing the script mid-run can't kill it ([#1142](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1142)); `_clear` falls back to ANSI when `tput clear` fails ([#1081](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1081)); `write_env` sets the shell variable too ([#1041](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1041)); drop the unused `RED` ([#1003](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/1003)).  - [#1198](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/pull/1198) [`868c4cf`](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/commit/868c4cf20cb7e08bc805c388ff1f305dbedd261a) Thanks [@mattpocock](https://github.com/mattpocock)! - `wizard` template fixes: `ask` uses Readline so arrow keys move the cursor ([#741](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/741)); `write_env` single-quotes values so spaces, `#`, `$` and quotes survive `source` and dotenv ([#770](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/770)); `open_url` prints the open-it-yourself warning when no browser opener exists ([#774](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/774)); `write_env` writes through a symlinked `.env` and keeps an existing file's mode ([#811](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/811)); `ask` and `ask_secret` fail at EOF instead of looping ([#852](https://github.com/mattpocock/skills/issues/852)).

### 新功能  - Kafka 连接在 SSL / SASL_SSL 下支持 TLS 客户端证书认证，可填写客户端证书与私钥的 PEM 路径 (contributed by @pengwow) (PR [#13894](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13894)) - MQ 消息浏览和订阅预览新增「解码」开关，勾选后尝试解析 Thrift 二进制载荷，默认显示原始内容 (contributed by @colabhoangbui2110) (PR [#13745](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13745)) - 设置中的代码片段列表支持多选、批量删除、搜索过滤和 Shift 连选 (contributed by @AndreasK79) (PR [#13885](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13885)) - SQL 编辑器支持行书签，用 Ctrl/Cmd+F2 或点击行号左侧书签栏切换，F2 / Shift+F2 在书签间跳转 (contributed by @PanSHIgg) (PR [#13057](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13057)) - SQL 文件面板中的非 SQL 文件改用系统默认应用打开，不再按 SQL 脚本读取 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12769](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12769)) - 导出菜单新增把本次全部结果集堆叠进单个 XLSX 工作表的动作，每个结果块依次输出标题行、列头行和数据行 (contributed by @HighBigSky) (PR [#13372](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13372)) - Oracle JDBC 连接支持进程列表 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#13711](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13711)) - 侧边栏连接名后可显示「服务器:端口@账号」小字，便于区分多服务器、多账号连接，默认关闭 (contributed by @caiyq925-cell) (PR [#13321](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13321)) - 新增「聚焦结果表格」和「聚焦 SQL 编辑器」两个可配置快捷键，可纯键盘完成查询、查看和复制结果 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14013](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14013)) - 数据表格「视图选项」新增默认导出方式和隐藏工具栏文字开关，默认导出方式同时作用于侧边栏、对象浏览器和数据库导出 (contributed by @efeire) (PR [#13854](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13854)) - MCP 服务器设置新增 Google Antigravity 客户端配置模板 (contributed by @wnyr) (PR [#13975](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13975)) - 设置 → 外观新增「标签双击行为」，可选择重命名标签或关闭标签 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#13357](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13357)) - 文档浏览器的筛选栏新增停用开关，可临时忽略筛选条件而不清空已填写的条件 (contributed by @spiritlord) (PR [#13812](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13812)) - 设置 → 编辑器新增「批量插入字段时包含注释」开关，关闭后批量勾选字段不再附带注释 (contributed by @JASON-QWeb) (PR [#14046](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14046)) - 存储过程与函数编辑支持保存前预览本次更新将生成的 SQL (contributed by @CSXFanMeng) (PR [#14088](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14088)) - MongoDB 查询编辑器支持运行 shell JavaScript 脚本，循环、变量和辅助函数可围绕 db 调用执行 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#11128](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11128)) - 插件新增 window.dbxPlugin.openUrl，经宿主确认后可打开插件声明过的外部网址 (contributed by @CSXFanMeng) (PR [#14089](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14089))  ### 改进  - 整行选中使用更深的颜色，与单元格选区覆盖的浅色提示区分开 (contributed by @mr-dadong) (PR [#7571](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/7571)) - 开启连接分组显示后，查询页的分组路径保留首尾分组并按剩余空间截断，连接详情浮窗也更紧凑 (contributed by @hanxuanyu) (PR [#13497](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13497)) - 「导出数据库」默认显示在数据库、Schema、表和视图的一级右键菜单 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#14112](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14112)) - 表结构编辑器的「复制其他表字段」补上 Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+C 快捷键 (contributed by @dd-xiaozhi) (PR [#13924](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13924)) - SQL 结果列头的悬停提示显示该列来源的限定列名，例如 public.users.email (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14066](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14066)) - 结果表格分页区在已知总页数时显示「共 X 页」，切换每页行数后同步更新 (contributed by @imcman) (PR [#13865](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13865)) - 结果状态栏同时显示查询返回的列数 (contributed by @heeeex100) (PR [#14100](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14100)) - Deepin 25 在 UI 缩放下 Canvas 结果文字不再模糊 (contributed by @arnann) (PR [#11961](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11961)) - 连接行的 PROD 标记与备注重新对齐 (contributed by @MingZhe1008) (PR [#13838](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13838))  ### 修复  - 修复只读连接误拒 SET @var、SET NAMES、SET search_path 等安全会话变量语句的问题 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#12768](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12768)) - 修复 PostgreSQL 数据库 SQL 导出丢失 PostGIS 空间值 SRID 和维度的问题 (contributed by @CN-Scars) (PR [#13914](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13914)) - 修复 Oracle 对象浏览器「全部」视图不显示表和视图注释的问题 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14099](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14099)) - 修复在新窗口保存查询后主窗口 SQL 库列表看不到文件的问题 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14003](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14003)) - 修复横向滚动 SQL 编辑器时行号与代码文本重叠的问题 (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11923](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11923)) - 修复未限定的表名无法获得外键连接条件建议的问题 (contributed by @saefulrahman) (PR [#12017](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/12017)) - 修复 Kingbase 视图 DDL 导出丢失视图和字段注释的问题 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14110](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14110)) - 修复状态栏 SQL 过长时挤掉总记录数显示的问题 (contributed by @onenewcode) (PR [#13272](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13272)) - 修复 MySQL 5.0 上索引元数据查询报 1054 错误、无法比较架构的问题 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#14101](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14101)) - 修复侧边栏操作错误提示被后续成功提示覆盖的问题 (contributed by @imcman) (PR [#13927](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13927)) - 修复 AI 回复被输出上限截断后缺少「继续生成」入口、输出 Token 超限直接报错的问题 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14079](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14079)) - 修复 PostgreSQL 分区很多的 schema 中表根显示不全、需要反复「加载更多」的问题 (contributed by @Tong-bit-art) (PR [#14002](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14002)) - 修复 SQL 诊断把客户端占位符 @packetCode 误报为未知列、把 MySQL 的 #{x} 及后续 SQL 当作注释的问题 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14036](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14036)) - 修复 SQL 文件树、内容搜索和快速打开不跟随符号链接的问题 (contributed by @lcq928) (PR [#14009](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14009)) - 修复打开 SQL 文件会替换当前查询、不新建查询标签的问题 (contributed by @imcman) (PR [#13928](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13928)) - 修复 MQTT 使用客户端证书认证时账号密码凭据丢失的问题 (contributed by @jingouzhui) (PR [#13816](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13816)) - 修复开启「自动选择侧边栏活动节点」后刚折叠的连接、库或表被重新展开的问题 (contributed by @ericbyz) (PR [#14071](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14071)) - 修复 Oracle PL/SQL 编译失败却显示成功、不提供行号和错误信息的问题 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#13996](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13996)) - 修复新建 MySQL 表无法选择表级字符集与排序规则的问题 (contributed by @imcman) (PR [#14032](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14032)) - 修复 Xugu 数据传输和导入时字符串字面量中的反斜杠被翻倍的问题 (contributed by @mapan0424) (PR [#13980](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13980)) - 修复 MySQL 表名含全角括号时编辑器截断表名、无法识别表和跳转的问题 (contributed by @Mo-Moore) (PR [#14116](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14116)) - 修复 SQL 关键字拼写错误会中断整条语句的引用诊断、错误标记不明显的问题 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#13982](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13982)) - 修复文件导入结束后已打开的表数据不刷新、需要重新打开的问题 (contributed by @gibbsfreeenergy) (PR [#13977](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13977)) - 修复 Oracle 宽表含 XMLTYPE 列时 date 解码反复失败的问题 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14004](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14004)) - 修复 SQL Server 三段式表名丢失数据库名、跨库查询误报未知表的问题 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#13959](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/13959)) - 修复 SQL Server 在 CROSS/OUTER 与 APPLY 之间有注释时丢失 APPLY 表补全上下文的问题 (contributed by @ericbyz) (PR [#14061](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14061)) - 修复 Dameng 低版本初始化作业环境后一直显示未就绪、重复报 -2124 对象已存在的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#14105](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14105)) - 修复 SQL Server 隐式存储过程调用不返回结果集的问题 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14072](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14072)) - 修复从备份恢复后 SQL 库不刷新、需要重启才能看到文件的问题 (contributed by @tiktalk945) (PR [#14073](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14073))
