### 2026-11-03T08:12:29Z [**tw93/Kaku**](https://github.com/tw93/Kaku)

```
<div align="center">   <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Kaku/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Kaku Logo" width="120" height="120" />   <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Kaku V0.28.0</h1>   <p><em>A fast, out-of-the-box terminal built for AI coding.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog 1. **Crash Fix**: Kaku no longer quits a minute after restoring a session with a long scrollback, which made it crash again on every reopen, and no longer quits when a menu action reaches a fullscreen window. 2. **Safe Updates**: Restart to Update always leaves a working Kaku.app in Applications, even when the update is interrupted halfway. 3. **Chinese Input**: The input method's candidate window now sits below the line you are typing, instead of covering half of the pinyin. 4. **Inline Images**: Images placed with kitty Unicode placeholders, such as the diagrams from Claude Code's figures plugin, now draw instead of showing boxes. 5. **Dim Text**: With a minimum contrast set, dim text stays dimmer than normal text on dark backgrounds, and a font named with a weight such as Light keeps that face for dim text. 6. **Folder Links**: Cmd+Click on a folder path opens it in Finder instead of VS Code. 7. **Remote Terminal**: Option+click an image path to view it over the page, hover previews open faster and larger, the close confirmation sits by its tab, and the page sends far less data while an agent is working. 8. **Freeze Recovery**: When keys and scrolling stop responding while menus still open, Kaku now tries to get its window running again by itself. 9. **App Icon**: The icon drops its baked-in shadows and sits on a darker ground, so it looks at home beside the system icons in the Dock.  ### 更新日志 1. **崩溃修复**：恢复带很长滚动记录的会话后，Kaku 不会再在一分钟后退出、重新打开又接着崩溃，菜单操作落到全屏窗口上时也不会再退出。 2. **安全更新**：重启以更新即使中途被打断，应用程序里也总会留着一个能用的 Kaku.app。 3. **中文输入**：输入法候选框现在出现在正在输入的那一行下方，不会再挡住一半拼音。 4. **终端图片**：用 kitty Unicode 占位符放置的图片，比如 Claude Code 的 figures 插件画的图，现在可以正常显示，不再是一排方框。 5. **暗淡文字**：设置了最低对比度时，深色背景上的暗淡文字仍然比普通文字暗，字体名带 Light 这类字重的，暗淡文字也会继续用这个字重。 6. **文件夹链接**：Cmd+点击文件夹路径会在访达里打开，不再用 VS Code 打开。 7. **远程终端**：按住 Option 点击图片路径可以直接在页面上看图，悬停预览更快也更大，关闭确认出现在对应标签旁边，Agent 干活时页面传输的数据也少了很多。 8. **卡死恢复**：按键和滚动都没反应、菜单却还能打开时，Kaku 现在会自己尝试让窗口恢复运行。 9. **应用图标**：图标去掉了原来的阴影，底色更深，放在程序坞里和系统图标更协调。  Special thanks to @multics, @sunbird89629 and @HiAriesZhou for their contributions to this release.  > https://github.com/tw93/Kaku 
```

### 2026-10-28T15:36:17Z [**rvben/rumdl**](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl)

```
 ### Fixed  - Upgrade pulldown-cmark to fix exponential parser event growth and duplicate   diagnostics after empty wikilink labels (RUSTSEC-2026-0414), and upgrade Tokio   to address its I/O safety advisory (RUSTSEC-2026-0386). Release publication now   requires a fresh dependency security audit, including during recovery runs. - MD087 recognizes inline suppression of heading-capitalization diagnostics,   avoiding false unused-disable warnings (#973). - MD022 enforces configured spacing below headings before lists and code fences,   including when MD031 or MD032 is disabled. Overlapping spacing fixes insert   the required blank lines only once (#971). - MD067 can safely reorder complete footnote definitions to match their first   references. Reordering is atomic, preserves surrounding content and directive   scope, and leaves ambiguous Markdown or MDX cases report-only (#969). - MD013 recognizes additional common abbreviations and titles, including numeric   references with formatted numbers, while preserving sentence boundaries after   ambiguous units and labels. Sentence-spacing checks share this behavior (#966). - MD013 preserves meaningful indentation inside multiline code spans following   a bare list marker when reflowing text.  - **security**: patch parser dependencies and gate release publication ([41d60e1](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/41d60e1ff6701c2c80ae515f7994b19793bbae80)) - **ci**: install only the tools required by parity checks ([ce014aa](https://github.com/rvben/rumdl/commit/ce014aab5cb71ae44327058a50972030c0555a88)) - **MD013**: preserve code-span indentation after bare list markers ### 2026-10-29T16:48:53Z [**kunkundi/crossdesk**](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk)
```

```
**新增功能与改进：**  1. 桌面端 IP 直连新增 IPv6 支持，并新增“监听所有 IP 接口”选项，默认开启。有线、Wi-Fi、VPN 和虚拟网卡可共用同一监听端口，方便在不同网络环境下建立直连会话。 2. Android、iOS 和鸿蒙移动端新增隐私屏控制，可在远程会话中开启或关闭被控电脑的隐私屏。新增允许本机快捷键解锁的设置，并支持在本机解除隐私屏后，由控制端重新锁定。 3. 桌面端与移动端统一支持 10 种界面语言，新增跟随系统语言能力。桌面端仍可手动选择语言，并保留已有语言设置。 4. 新增与 Web 客户端的双向文本剪贴板同步支持，方便在浏览器与被控电脑之间复制、粘贴文本。 5. 移动端网络状态新增实际编码器和解码器信息；桌面端与移动端的较长编解码器名称支持自动滚动显示。同步优化移动端控制菜单、隐私屏状态图标及提示布局。  **问题修复：**  1. 修复 Windows、Linux 被控电脑未连接鼠标时，远程光标可能不显示的问题。 2. 修复空闲音频播放设备持续占用，导致 Windows 无法自动休眠的问题。 3. 修复移动端系统输入法向 Windows 锁屏等安全桌面输入文本的问题，改善远程登录密码输入体验。 4. 修复 Windows IP 直连身份文件的所有者与权限问题，改善管理员权限运行场景下的兼容性。 5. 修复移动端屏幕方向控制，使横屏锁定仅在开启“连接后自动横屏”时生效；被控电脑锁屏时隐藏不可用的隐私屏操作，并仅对 Windows 被控端显示专用快捷键。 6. 恢复 macOS 安装时重置系统权限的逻辑，以清理旧版本授权记录，安装后需要重新授权；修复系统权限列表中的小尺寸图标花屏，完善应用与安装包的签名、公证流程。 7. 修复近期连接提示残留及下拉列表内容与滚动条重叠的问题，并改善 Windows、iOS 等平台的构建兼容性。  **使用提示：**  - IPv6 直连需使用支持该功能的桌面客户端。指定端口时请使用 `[IPv6地址]:端口` 格式，例如 `[2001:db8::20]:52888`；链路本地地址还需指定控制端的网卡作用域。双方网络需允许 TCP 和 UDP 互通。 - “监听所有 IP 接口”默认开启，可在“设置 → 常规 → 网络连接”中关闭，恢复按物理网卡 IP 分别配置端口。 - 移动端隐私屏和 Windows 安全桌面文本输入建议两端同时升级；Windows 锁屏输入还需安装并启动新版 CrossDesk Service。 - Web 剪贴板同步需搭配支持该功能的 Web 客户端，当前支持文本内容。  ---  **New Features and Improvements:**  1. Added IPv6 support for desktop direct IP connections and a “Listen on all IP interfaces” option, enabled by default. Ethernet, Wi-Fi, VPN, and virtual interfaces can share one listening port. 2. Added privacy screen controls to Android, iOS, and HarmonyOS clients. Added a setting to allow or disable local shortcut unlocking, and support for remotely restoring the privacy screen after it has been unlocked locally. 3. Unified desktop and mobile localization with support for 10 interface languages and system language detection. Desktop clients also support manual language selection and preserve existing preferences. 4. Added bidirectional text clipboard synchronization with compatible Web clients, making it easier to copy and paste text between the browser and the remote computer. 5. Mobile network statistics now show the actual encoder and decoder in use. Long codec names scroll automatically on desktop and mobile. Refined mobile controls, privacy screen status icons, and hint layouts.  **Bug Fixes:**  1. Fixed missing remote cursors on Windows and Linux hosts without a connected mouse. 2. Fixed idle audio playback devices preventing Windows from automatically sleeping. 3. Fixed text input from mobile system keyboards on Windows secure desktops, including the lock screen, improving remote password entry. 4. Fixed ownership and permission handling for Windows direct IP identity files, improving compatibility when running with administrator privileges. 5. Fixed mobile orientation handling so landscape locking applies only when automatic landscape mode is enabled. Unavailable privacy controls are hidden on locked desktops, and Windows-specific shortcuts are shown only for Windows hosts. 6. Restored macOS permission resets during installation to clear authorization records from earlier versions. Permissions must be granted again after installation. Fixed corrupted small icons in the macOS permission list and improved application and installer signing and notarization. 7. Fixed stale recent-connection tooltips and dropdown items overlapping scrollbars, and improved build compatibility on Windows, iOS, and other platforms.  **Usage Notes:**  - IPv6 direct connections require compatible desktop clients. Use `[IPv6 address]:port` when specifying a port, such as `[2001:db8::20]:52888`. Link-local addresses also require the controller’s network interface scope. Both TCP and UDP traffic must be allowed between endpoints. - “Listen on all IP interfaces” is enabled by default. Disable it under Settings → General → Network to restore separate port settings for physical interface addresses. - Update both endpoints for mobile privacy controls and Windows secure desktop text input. Windows lock-screen input also requires an updated, running CrossDesk Service. - Web clipboard synchronization requires a compatible Web client and currently supports text.  **完整变更 / Full Changelog：** [[v1.7.3-20261026…v1.7.4-20261030](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.3-20261026...v1.7.4-20261030)](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.3-20261026...v1.7.4-20261030) 
```

### 2026-10-30T23:38:25Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v1.16.0  Install from openspec.dev, catch more spec mistakes in `validate`, and a new Node.js floor for npm installs.  ### Breaking  - **Node.js 22.12.0 or newer for npm installs** - Node 20 reached end-of-life on April 30, 2026. The standalone binary, the default install since v1.15.0, doesn't use Node, and Homebrew installs and manages its own Node. If you can't upgrade, switch to the standalone binary: `curl -fsSL https://openspec.dev/cli/install.sh | sh` (Windows: `irm https://openspec.dev/cli/install.ps1 | iex`).  ### New  - **Install from openspec.dev** - `curl -fsSL https://openspec.dev/cli/install.sh | sh` (Windows: `irm https://openspec.dev/cli/install.ps1 | iex`). Release files are now `.tar.gz` archives (`.zip` on Windows). - **Scenario step check** - `openspec validate` warns when a `Scenario:` has no WHEN/GIVEN step or no THEN step, in change deltas and main specs. Plain `validate` and `archive` are unchanged; `--strict` fails on it. - **Store suggestion** - When a command finds no OpenSpec root and you have registered stores, the error names the store whose id matches your repo's `origin` name, with the `--store` flag and `store:` line that select it. It only suggests; it never picks a store for you.  ### Fixed  - **Invalid config.yaml** - `openspec validate` now exits 1 when `openspec/config.yaml` can't be read or isn't valid YAML, instead of passing while every rule in it is ignored. Other commands still warn and carry on. - **zsh completion with ZDOTDIR** - `openspec completion install zsh` writes to `$ZDOTDIR/.zshrc` when `ZDOTDIR` is an absolute path, the file zsh actually reads. If completion never worked for you, run the install again. `completion uninstall zsh` also cleans up a block an earlier install left in `~/.zshrc`. - **Inline code fences** - A line starting with triple-backtick inline code (` npm test `) no longer opens a phantom code fence that hid every requirement after it. - **Unspaced headers** - `validate` now reports duplicate or misplaced main-spec requirements written as `###Requirement:`, matching how archive reads them.  ## New Contributors  * @micromagicman made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2077 * @IvanFeric made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2107 * @artemasmith made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2079  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.15.0...v1.16.0 
```

### 2026-10-31T12:22:39Z [**Gaurav-Gosain/tuios**](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)

```
tuios 0.12.1 fixes a crash on the task board and makes right-click Preview work from any pane. The rail now shows the names your agents set. The drop preview is now only a frame by default.  ## Install  bash brew install tuios                     # Homebrew (macOS, Linux) yay -S tuios-bin                       # AUR (tuios-web-bin for the web server) nix run github:Gaurav-Gosain/tuios     # Nix curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/main/install.sh | bash go install github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/cmd/tuios@v0.12.1 docker run -it --rm ghcr.io/gaurav-gosain/tuios:v0.12.1   A binary installed by the script or from an archive updates with `tuios update`. Then run `tuios restart-server` to move the daemon to the new build. The restart stops the programs in your panes. Each session comes back with its layout, its pane history and new shells.  ## Before you upgrade  - **The drop preview is only a frame.** While you drag a pane in BSP, the preview now draws a frame with no tint inside. Set `appearance.drop_preview = "fill"` to get the tint back ([#1408](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1408)). - **A correction to the v0.12.0 notes.** `tuios restart-server` does not apply the SSH fix from v0.12.0, because the limits are in the `tuios ssh` process. If you run `tuios ssh` where other people can reach it, restart that process too ([#1392](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1392)).  ## Fixes  - The task board no longer crashes when the selection reaches the folded Done heading ([#1409](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1409)). - Right-click a file and pick Preview now works from any focus. In v0.12.0 the preview closed at once unless the rail had the keyboard and its cursor was on a file ([#1387](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1387)). - `next_session`, `prev_session` and `switch_session_N` now follow the order of the rail's rows, and the number beside a row is the key that opens it. Thanks to @zerone0x, and to @maciej-lech for the report ([#1300](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1300)). - The sessions section scrolls to a new or switched-to session when the list is grouped. Thanks to @maciej-lech for the report ([#1401](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1401)). - The `files` control in the rail's footer now opens the files section when `appearance.sidebar.sections` leaves it out. You can keep the section hidden by default and open it when you need it. Thanks to @maciej-lech for the report ([#1407](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1407)). - Two data races are gone: one on a pane's folder report and one on `OS.SetTheme` ([#1402](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1402), [#1403](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1403)).  ## New  - **Agent names in the rail.** A row now shows the title that an agent set, such as the name from `claude -n` or `/rename`. A custom name still comes first. When several agents show only the program, each row has a suffix that stays with its pane. Thanks to @zoneblaze for the report and the first proposal ([#1388](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1388)). - **Open a review on the file list.** Set `[review] focus = "files"`. Thanks to @maciej-lech ([#1378](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1378)). - **More surfaces follow your look.** With `appearance.pane_overlays` on, the plugin approval dialog, the scrollback browser and the copy-mode search bar take the pane look. The scrollback browser and its help modal also follow `appearance.border_style`. Thanks to @abhijit-s ([#1096](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1096), [#1108](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1108)). - **Windows.** A pane opens on the `conpty.dll` and `OpenConsole.exe` beside `tuios.exe` when both files are there. With them, a pane's sixel images reach tuios. Without them, nothing changes. Thanks to @badmagick329 ([#1389](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1389)). - **For the apps.** The new `list-routes` verb gives the addresses a phone can reach this machine on, and `queue-prompt` takes a `client_id`, so a resend queues once ([#1380](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1380), [#1379](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1379)). The GUI bridge reports the queue, the draft and the shelf, and it can read an agent's transcript ([#1377](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1377)).  ## Development  - The macOS development checks pass. Thanks to @syz51 ([#1394](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1394)). - Several tests that failed in the nightly race run no longer give false failures ([#1404](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1404), [#1405](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1405), [#1406](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/1406)).  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.12.0...v0.12.1 
```

### 2026-11-02T13:54:23Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
# GPUI Kit v0.9.0  GPUI Kit 0.9.0 adds editor and Diff decorations, an application-level icon resolver, theme-configurable cursor blinking and an input edits event, along with many accessibility, menu, table and text-rendering fixes. The default backend moves to gpui-pre 0.3.11 and the optional GPUI Fast backend to 0.1.13.  This is a minor release because public theme structs are now `#[non_exhaustive]`, which breaks struct literals outside the crate. See **Breaking changes** below for migration.  ## Editor and text input  - **Gutter and overview ruler decorations**: `GutterDecorationCollection` marks lines in a strip before the line numbers, and `OverviewRulerDecorationCollection` marks them on the vertical scrollbar track, e.g. for lines added, modified or deleted by version control. Highlight themes now read the `created`, `modified` and `deleted` status colors. ([#3704](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3704)) - **Edit events**: `InputEditsEvent` is emitted with each `InputEvent::Change` and carries the batch of `InputEdit`s, each with the document after it, for Input, Textarea and Editor. ([#3751](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3751)) - **Cursor blinking**: `Theme::cursor_blink` with `CursorBlink::steady()`, `CursorBlink::blinking(interval)` and `with_idle_timeout` configures the caret for every input. The default stays at 500 ms with no idle timeout. ([#3713](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3713)) - **Native touch selection**: `set_native_touch_selection` leaves selection handles and the edit menu to the platform; `set_selected_text_range` is now implemented so the platform or IME can move the selection. ([#3723](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3723)) - Restore backward selections through `set_selected_range`, reuse measured wraps during live resize, keep hover working across the whole visible editor after scrolling, and paint editor popups above dialogs. ([#3756](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3756), [#3691](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3691), [#3734](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3734), [#3700](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3700))  ## Diff  - **Line decorations and gutter markers**: `Diff::decorations` paints backgrounds on line ranges with `DiffDecoration`, and `Diff::gutter_markers` with `render_gutter_marker` places interactive markers beside lines without changing the selection. ([#3703](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3703)) - Keep the vertical scrollbar at the viewport edge when `soft_wrap` is off. ([#3739](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3739))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - Render reference-style Markdown images, continue alphabetic ordered-list numbering after Z, and preserve list body width in fitted containers. ([#3683](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3683), [#3722](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3722), [#3759](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3759)) - Draw marked text in a color that reads on its background. ([#3746](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3746)) - Fix an application shutdown deadlock during layout cleanup, and avoid resolving the window selection during paint in selectable text. ([#3697](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3697), [#3699](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3699))  ## Components  - **Icon**: `set_icon_resolver` lets the application choose the path every icon is drawn from, including icons drawn inside components, for runtime icon themes. `resolve_icon_path` serves custom components that draw with `svg()`. ([#3633](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3633)) - **Menu**: submenu rows are activatable, and a submenu opens on the left only when the right side has no room. ([#3714](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3714), [#3736](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3736)) - **Table**: column resize follows the point where the drag started. ([#3732](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3732)) - **Combobox**: correct filtered selection events and confirmation. ([#3449](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3449)) - **VirtualList**: rebuild cached geometry when the axis or gap changes, and use the height constraint when a horizontal list infers its height. ([#3719](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3719), [#3720](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3720)) - **MessageScroller**: the scrollbar reaches the end of padded content. **Popover** clears focus on close when nothing had focus at open. **Toolbar** ends its wrap search at the first repeated stop. ([#3615](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3615), [#3718](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3718), [#3716](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3716)) - **Settings**: show an empty state for unmatched searches; the sidebar follows scrolling. ([#3701](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3701)) - **Motion**: directly built easing curves with nothing to sample behave as linear. ([#3721](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3721)) - **Speech**: `SpeechWaveform` gains a liquid appearance, `SpeechButton` shows the voice while capturing, and macOS privacy access no longer falls on the terminal. ([#3725](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3725), [#3726](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3726), [#3730](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3730))  ## Accessibility  - Tables give the focused element the grid role and move assistive-technology focus with the selected row or cell. ([#3727](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3727), [#3758](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3758)) - Menus report checked and unchecked items correctly; command palette rows are named after their item; icon-only close buttons have names. ([#3729](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3729), [#3761](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3761), [#3728](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3728), [#3741](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3741))  ## Localization and testing  - Add Russian (`ru`) translations. ([#3745](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3745)) - Test support can observe painted text and alternative text, and Toolbar is registered for snapshot queries. ([#3724](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3724), [#3717](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3717))  ## Breaking changes  **Theme structs are `#[non_exhaustive]`** ([#3713](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3713), [#3704](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3704)). Struct literals, including `..Default::default()`, no longer compile outside the defining crate. Later field additions will not break callers again.  - gpui-base: `Theme`, `SemanticThemeTokens`, `ColorTokens`, `RadiusTokens`, `SpacingTokens`, `TypographyTokens`, `TextStyleToken`, `ShadowTokens`, `ResizableTheme`, `ListSettings` - gpui-component: `Theme`, `ThemeColor`, `ThemeToken`, `ThemeSet`, `ThemeConfig`, `ThemeConfigColors`, `Semantic*Config`, `MotionTokens`, `HighlightTheme`, `HighlightThemeStyle`, `SyntaxColors`, `SheetSettings`, `NotificationSettings`  Start from `Default::default()` or the existing constructor (`ColorTokens::light`, `ShadowTokens::elevations`, …) and assign fields:  diff -let theme = Theme { radius: px(4.), ..Default::default() }; +let mut theme = Theme::default(); +theme.radius = px(4.);   `DisplayMap` is no longer `UnwindSafe` / `RefUnwindSafe`.  ## Upstream GPUI compatibility  gpui-pre 0.3.11 / gpui-fast 0.1.13 ([#3766](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3766)):  - `Window::text_system()` returns `&Rc<WindowTextSystem>` instead of `&Arc`, and `WindowTextSystem` is no longer `Send`/`Sync`. - `Animation` reads the executor clock instead of `Instant::now()`, so it follows the test clock. Tests that slept on the wall clock should use `advance_clock`.  Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback!   ## What's Changed * input: Reuse measured wraps during live resize by @T-miracle in ### 2026-11-02T02:49:38Z [**Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP**](https://github.com/Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP)
```

```
## What's Changed  ### Features - **`idamcp-hub tui <path>`** — an interactive r2-style terminal UI over the same headless idalib workers. Disassembly/pseudocode pane toggle (`p`), seek by name or address (`g`), xrefs (`x`), follow xref (`f`), rename (`n`), comment (`m`), call graph (`G`), and an ASCII CFG rendered from `get_basic_blocks` (`b`), plus a function sidebar tree and an instance status bar. Built with [textual](https://github.com/Textualize/textual) as an **optional dependency**: `pip install "idamcp-hub[tui]"` or `uvx --from idamcp-hub --extra tui idamcp-hub tui <path>`. - **`idamcp-hub dump <path>`** — non-interactive assembly/pseudocode export in the [idalib-dump](https://github.com/19h/idalib-dump) style: name-regex filter (`-f`), `--asm-only`/`--pseudo-only`, `-o FILE` output with failure counting. No textual dependency — plain text rendering, CI-friendly.  ### Design Both run **on the host (uvx) side**, never inside a worker: the TUI and dump talk to workers through the normal bridge client and render from the existing tool catalog (`disasm`, `decompile`, `xrefs_to`, `list_functions`, `get_basic_blocks`, `callgraph`, `rename_function`, `set_comment`). Workers keep their zero-third-party-dependency stdlib bridge; multi-instance routing reuses the same stateless `instance_id` rules as MCP calls.  ### Tests - `test_tui.py`: dump parse/render layer exercised against canned tool outputs (no IDA needed). - The textual app is pilot-tested headlessly against a live worker (disasm/pseudo/xrefs/CFG panes all verified rendering). - Full suite: **238 passed**.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Captain-AI-Hub/IDA-MCP/compare/v1.0.1...v1.1.0 
```

### 2026-11-03T16:55:41Z [**AChep/keyguard-app**](https://github.com/AChep/keyguard-app)

```
### New - Vault: Added import from other password managers (#1650). - Sync: Added mTLS support (#819). - Desktop: Added an interface scale option to Appearance settings. - SSH agent: Added SSH bookmark fields for auto-configuring ~/.ssh/config.  ### Improvements - Passkeys: Passkeys now support the WebAuthn PRF extension (#1651). - Attachments: Attachments can now be dragged out into file managers. - Attachments: Attachment preview now includes a JSON tree view. - Attachments: Attachment preview now supports .gz files. - SSH agent: Agent approval requests now support configuring a timeout duration. - SSH agent: Agent requests can now be configured to never ask for explicit approval. - UI: Approval requests for CLI access now include a request log. - GPG: Dropping files into GPG tools' tiles now selects the operation with the file pre-filled. - Data: Updated translations.  ### Fixes - Android: Fixed popup autofill failing to show suggested logins while inline autofill works fine (#1652). - GPG: Fixed the agent so it supports s2k_count, UPDATESTARTUPTTY and SCD replies. 
```

### 2026-11-02T20:32:06Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - The options page displays a warning when the extension is not allowed to access local files, with the steps to allow it: "Access local files on your computer" in the permissions of the extension since Firefox 153, where it is off by default, and "Allow access to file URLs" in Chromium-based browsers. Without it, the infobar is not displayed on saved pages and pages opened from the filesystem cannot be saved. The help page and the FAQ describe it too (#2014) - New hidden option `embeddedScript` (core 1.9.0), set from the options editor: JavaScript code inserted at the end of the saved page, kept even though the scripts of the page are removed. Combined with a custom stylesheet that keeps hidden elements, it lets a user restore the tabs of a saved page, for example - Help page: the examples of the time template variables use ":" instead of "_" (#2041 by @lstngr), and the ISO variables are documented as UTC (#2042) - Translations: Russian reviewed (#2039 by @solokot), Swedish reviewed (#2040 by @NickWick13), German label of the proof of existence option fixed, missing translations and spelling mistakes fixed in several locales  ### Fixes  - Filename templates (core 1.8.1): the spaces written in the template are kept instead of being collapsed into a single space; tabs, line breaks and other whitespace characters are still replaced with spaces, one for one (#2042) - Filename templates (core 1.8.1): a substitution rule that removes characters, and the "replace emojis with text" option, no longer leave two spaces around the removed character or a space before the file extension - Filename templates (core 1.8.1): a folder name ending with a dot no longer loses its separator ("docs./file" was saved as "docs._file"), a ".." segment is removed as a whole segment instead of wherever "../" appears ("Loading.../page" was saved as "Loading.page"), and trailing dots and spaces are removed from every folder name; the filename preview on the options page follows the same rules - A missing image is no longer requested a second time when its `currentSrc` is the same URL as its `src`, which doubled the network wait on slow or unavailable images (gildas-lormeau/single-file-core#11 by @pirate) 
```

### 2026-11-01T22:16:50Z [**dani-garcia/vaultwarden**](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden)

```
## Security Fixes This release contains security fixes for the following advisories. We strongly advise updating as soon as possible. - Website icons [[GHSA-x9cq-f669-vrr4]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-x9cq-f669-vrr4) (**Medium**, 5.3) - Emergency access [[GHSA-9pjj-f9g5-3x4c]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-9pjj-f9g5-3x4c) (**Medium**, 4.3)  These are private for now, pending CVE assignment and publishing at a later date.  ## Note This update is required for web vault 2026.7.1+ to turn two-step login methods on or off, and for 2026.9.0+ to take over an account through emergency access. Please update before reporting any issues with them.  ## Upgrade notes - **Web vault:** the bundled web vault is now v2026.10.1. If you serve your own web vault older than v2026.7.1, you might have compatibility issues. - **Configuration:** invalid values now stop startup with an error. Numeric options have tighter types, so negative or too large values are rejected, for example above 65535 for `LOGIN_RATELIMIT_MAX_BURST`, `TRASH_AUTO_DELETE_DAYS`, `EVENTS_DAYS_RETAIN` or `SMTP_TIMEOUT`, or above 255 for `EMAIL_ATTEMPTS_LIMIT`. The rate limit `*_SECONDS` and `*_MAX_BURST` options, `DATABASE_TIMEOUT` and `DATABASE_IDLE_TIMEOUT` must be at least 1, `DATABASE_MIN_CONNS` can't be larger than `DATABASE_MAX_CONNS`, and `DUO_CONTEXT_PURGE_SCHEDULE` and `PURGE_INCOMPLETE_SSO_AUTH` must be empty or a valid cron expression.  ## What's Changed * Close WebSocket connections that stop responding by @Cassian433 in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7807 * Validate the Duo and SSO purge schedules on startup by @NotAFlightRisk in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7819 * docs: fix "runnning" typo in playwright README by @ump45nose in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7787 * docs: fix Playwright test env link by @dajiaohuang in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7739 * Fix group import to update groups on data change by @Jogius in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7671 * [Web 2026.9.0] Support ### 2026-11-02T09:55:27Z [**ryan4yin/nix-config**](https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config)
```

```
> **Sandboxed desktop apps, hardened homelab, and udev in place of oneshots.** 117 commits across 25 PRs, 297 files, +8.8k/−2.5k lines in 14 days — mostly authored by agents: **Claude Code (claude-opus-5-5)** and **DeepSeek Harness (deepseek-flash)**, with some work from GPT-6 Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol. These notes were drafted by Claude Code (claude-opus-5-5).  ## Highlights  - **nixpak sandboxes for desktop apps**: a unified sandbox with a cleared environment, no system bus, and isolated network now wraps Foliate, Krita, Bambu Studio, OrcaSlicer and more, with per-app policy checks (#490, #491, #493–#497). - **Homelab hardening**: SSH, services and the audit trail on the homelab are hardened, k3s pods can no longer reach etcd's client port, and roots with compromised eMudhra intermediates are distrusted (#479, #489). - **Power monitoring and wake on demand**: homelab power draw is monitored, `aquamarine` suspends when idle and wakes over LAN, and the mini PCs get Wake-on-LAN too (#501, #503, #509). - **Builders and caching**: `aquamarine` serves as a remote builder for every physical host, and `youko` runs a pull-through cache for two Nix upstreams (#499, #500). - **Less imperative glue**: hardware tweaks move from custom oneshots to udev rules and tmpfiles, LAN exporters and whisper-asr are socket-activated with FreeBind, and many stale pins, overlays and workarounds are retired.  ## Features  - Sandbox desktop apps with nixpak: unify the sandboxes, clear the environment, drop the system bus and isolate the network; add Foliate, Krita, Bambu Studio and OrcaSlicer (#490, #491, #494, #495, #497). - Package WeChat natively from nixpkgs with app-scoped data (#492). - Monitor homelab power and reduce `aquamarine`'s idle draw; suspend it after idle and wake it over LAN (#501, #503). - Enable Wake-on-LAN on the mini PCs (#509). - Use `aquamarine` as a remote builder on the remaining physical hosts (#500), and add a pull-through cache for two Nix upstreams on `youko` (#499). - Track `linuxPackages_latest` on `aquamarine` (#478). - Alert when the last successful backup goes stale. - Enable on-demand VRR for games in niri. - Add ipython data helpers without persisted history (#510). - Fetch npm through npmmirror, persist `~/models` on `ai`, re-add gitleaks, and install python313 on every NixOS host. - Add Japanese and Korean locale font fallbacks and prefer Noto CJK family names.  ## Fixes  - Harden homelab SSH, services and the audit trail; mount `/etc/ssh` in initrd so key changes apply on boot (#489). - Deny pod sources the etcd client port 2379 in k3s, and distrust roots with compromised eMudhra intermediates (#479). - Catch up restic backups missed while a host was off, date `youko`'s store snapshots by name, and drop stale btrfs snapshots before taking a new one. - Apply the declarative OpenRGB off profile at boot and reapply it after resume; persist OpenRGB settings (#502, #508). - Keep the mihomo proxy up when the resolved hook times out. - Route Zoom screen sharing through xdg-desktop-portal-wlr 0.8.2, and let untitled Proton windows go fullscreen. - Keep displays awake during gamepad use (#480), and pin `ai`'s 4K monitor at 144 Hz. - Fix Colmena to deploy the systems `nixosConfigurations` evaluates, and run k3s MicroVM guests from their own entries (#483, #484). - Socket-activate LAN exporters and whisper-asr with FreeBind instead of waiting for the network.  ## Refactors & cleanup  - Replace custom oneshots with udev rules: R9700 power limit, `shoukei` charge cap, RAPL access, HDD spindown, and the Insta360 Link reset on resume. - Move `youko` setup into tmpfiles and native NixOS modules (homepage, caddy wallpapers, `/data-ro` mountpoints). - Get NUR packages from the nur-ryan4yin overlay, link mihomo geodata from nixpkgs, and route VR headset audio with a WirePlumber hook. - Drop retired pins and workarounds (smartctl-exporter, tuios flake, NVMe and nvidia-drm params, `pcie_aspm=off`, the Comodo root blacklist), replace redundant `mkForce`, and remove VS Code and the riscv64 module. - Update nixpkgs to e62746b and refresh flake inputs (#488); bump `dsh-nu` to 0.6.0.  ## Docs  - Centralize documentation under `docs/`, refresh the workarounds table, record the 2026-10-22 security audit follow-up, and tighten the agent rules.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/compare/v0.19.0...v0.20.0 
```

### 2026-11-01T13:49:08Z [**labring/FastGPT**](https://github.com/labring/FastGPT)

```
## 升级指南  ### 配置变更  - 源码部署的 Node.js 版本须满足 `^22.23.4 || ^24.22.1 || ^26.12.1`，升级前请先确认运行环境符合该范围。 - `DEFAULT_TEAM_BASIC_PERMISSIONS_ENABLED` 仍默认 `false`；启用时，新团队默认全员组除应用、技能和知识库创建权限外，还会获得 API Key 创建权限，已有团队不自动补授。 - Agent 沙盒活跃实例总上限改为通过 `AGENT_SANDBOX_MAX` 配置，默认 100，应用会话与技能编辑共用额度；原 `AGENT_SANDBOX_MAX_EDIT_DEBUG` 不再使用。新增 `AGENT_SANDBOX_MAX_PER_TEAM` 设置单团队上限，默认不限制。商业版管理台中的配额配置优先于环境变量。 - OpenSandbox 新增 `AGENT_SANDBOX_CPU_OVERCOMMIT_FACTOR` 与 `AGENT_SANDBOX_MEMORY_OVERCOMMIT_FACTOR`，默认均为 1，取值须不小于 1。仅在 Kubernetes 运行态且 OpenSandbox Server 不低于 v0.2.1 时生效，降低资源预留但保持资源上限不变，仅影响新建或重建的沙盒。 - 商业版管理台新增「向量检索最大并发数」（`systemEnv.vectorRecallConcurrency`），允许配置 0–1000，默认 0 表示不限制。正数限制所有服务实例、所有向量库的共享召回并发；开源版不启用此限制，无对应环境变量。 - 新增 `AUXILIARY_GENERATION_SHOW_RUN_DETAIL`，默认 `false`，控制技能、Agent 和工作流辅助生成的运行详情入口是否展示。 - 应用市场默认地址（`MARKETPLACE_URL`）更新为 `https://v3.marketplace.fastgpt.cn`，显式配置的自定义地址继续使用原值。  ### 镜像更新  - 更新 fastgpt-app（FastGPT 主服务）镜像 tag: v4.17.3 - 更新 fastgpt-pro（FastGPT 商业版）镜像 tag: v4.17.3 - 更新 fastgpt-plugin 镜像 tag: v1.2.0 - 更新 fastgpt-code-sandbox 镜像 tag(后续都独立发布，按需更新): v5.0.0 - 更新 AI Proxy 镜像 tag: v0.7.7  ### 升级脚本  系统先完成启动必需任务及其前置依赖链，各 App 实例重载模型后启动业务。以下 3 个新增任务随后在后台自动执行，共享集群最多 3 个并发名额，失败不会阻断无依赖任务；可在管理页查看结果，修复失败数据后重试。  - **回填沙盒实例团队归属**（`20261009_backfill_agent_sandbox_team_id`）：根据来源应用或技能补齐历史实例的团队归属，用于团队配额统计；来源已删除的孤儿记录跳过并计数。 - **回填目录祖先路径**（`20261022_backfill_folder_ancestor_ids`）：重建应用、工具及知识库目录路径，清理非文件夹资源的残留字段。直接挂在其他类型目录树下的文件夹移到本团队根目录，并清理旧父级继承权限；更高层的跨树祖先路径在边界处截断。缺失父级、跨团队或成环等异常记录保留为失败项，修复后重试。 - **迁移系统模型价格至百万单位**（`20261025_token_prices_per_million`）：将历史系统模型价格换算并写入 `pricing`，保留旧字段原值，跳过已有合法 `pricing` 的模型；团队模型不参与迁移。  ### 兼容说明  - **root 激活与密码**：移除 `DEFAULT_ROOT_PSW`，启动不再覆盖已有 root 密码。新建 root 初始密码为 `123456`，首次登录需设置新密码；历史 root 未保存 `status` 时需使用原密码登录并完成激活。日常改密在账号信息页操作，忘记密码时使用 root 密码重置脚本，并显式提供新密码。 - **模型计价单位**：LLM 使用 `pricing.tiers`，其他模型使用 `pricing.unitPrice`。Token 单价与阶梯 `maxInputTokens` 上限改为百万 Tokens，TTS 改为每百万字符，STT 仍按每 60 秒，单价最多保留两位小数。升级后确认后台迁移成功并核对系统模型价格；未迁入 `pricing` 的系统模型暂按免费处理，团队模型免费。 - **工作流编排校验**：画布、调试与辅助生成统一遵循[编排规则](/guide/build/workflow/rules)，禁止纯串行回环、跨工具流程连线及多个调用节点共用同一工具入口，允许由有效条件出口控制的回流。工具子流程可读取调用者上游，但不可引用调用者自身尚未产生的输出。旧工作流保留已有连线并可保存草稿，调试和发布前需修正不符合规则的连线。 - **知识库管理权限**：修改知识库基本信息、模型配置，以及文件名称、标签、禁用状态等自身属性需要可管理（`manage`）权限；内容操作继续使用原有编辑权限。移动文件时同时修改属性，也需要该文件的管理权限。 - **知识库导出**：`/api/core/dataset/exportAll` 仅支持单个知识库，目录导出需逐库请求；开启文件级权限时，仅导出当前成员可读文件。 - **对话文件类型**：附件分类新增 `document` 与 `unknown`；旧 `file` 入参和历史记录继续兼容，并按文件名、链接及响应信息归类。自定义客户端应兼容新分类，不再仅用 `file` 判断文档。 - **分页与错误响应**：通用分页将超过 500 的 `pageSize` 按 500 处理，部分接口仍有更小限制；同时传入 `offset` 和 `pageNum` 时优先使用 `offset`，包括 `offset=0`，非法偏移量不回退为页码。被识别为用户配置错误的 API 请求返回 HTTP 400，接入方需按失败响应处理。  ## 🚀 新增内容  1. **[工作流与 Agent]** 商业版新增[工作流辅助生成](https://doc.fastgpt.io/zh-CN/guide/build/workflow/assisted-generation)，通过自然语言确认方案后，由同一 Agent 连续搭建、校验和修复并交付候选版本，中断后可根据已保存的对话、计划和任务状态继续。候选版本卡片支持赞踩反馈，需手动应用并检查、调试后再保存发布；使用时需有应用编辑权限，并启用辅助生成与沙盒能力。 2. **[工作流与 Agent]** 用户选择节点及表单的单选、多选字段支持变量引用动态选项，动态选项为空时返回明确错误。 3. **[工作流与 Agent]** 密码输入、工具密钥与 HTTP/MCP 鉴权请求头支持变量引用，可在运行时使用上游节点或应用变量提供凭据。 4. **[模型与计费]** 支持团队成员配置专属 AI 模型与渠道，完善资源隔离、鉴权及 AI Proxy 集成；团队所有者可批量替换失效的语言、语音播放和重排模型引用，覆盖知识库、应用及历史版本，并展示处理统计。空值、动态引用与索引模型不参与替换。 5. **[团队与权限]** 新增账号待激活与首次设置密码流程，root 可在账号信息页改密；激活或改密后保留当前登录会话并清理其他会话。商业版管理台可按全部、活跃、待激活、禁用和注销状态筛选用户。 6. **[团队与权限]** 创建应用、工具与知识库文件夹时可选择是否继承权限；资源目录导航支持沿父级路径访问被授权的子资源，父目录仅作为导航入口，不额外授予管理权限。 7. **[团队与权限]** 团队审计补充知识库训练任务重试、删除、切换索引模型，以及资源恢复权限继承等操作记录，保留资源路径和模型标识，支持按事件类型筛选。 8. **[对话体验]** 支持恢复输入草稿与已成功上传的附件引用，按成员、应用、会话隔离；当前浏览器标签页最多保留 10 份草稿，自最后编辑起保留 1 小时，发送被正式受理后清除。 9. **[对话体验]** Markdown 支持有限 HTML 渲染，完善富文本组件交互。 10. **[工具与渠道]** 系统插件的名称、简介、子工具、参数、密钥 Schema 与 README 支持多语言展示，已有工作流节点与 Agent 固定工具保留添加时的语言快照。 11. **[工具与渠道]** MCP 与外部渠道统一接入团队对话 QPM 限制，超限返回额度和等待时间，外部渠道去重后计数。依赖服务异常时记录日志并跳过检查，避免故障阻断对话。 12. **[系统与运维]** Agent 沙盒支持系统总配额与团队配额，OpenSandbox Kubernetes 运行态可独立配置 CPU、内存超卖倍率，控制活跃实例数量与资源预留。 13. **[系统与运维]** 新增按请求头 `x-fastgpt-profile: 1` 启用的性能采集，汇总阶段耗时、Mongo 操作及进程指标并支持 OTel 上报。 14. **[知识库与文件]** 新增 [OXR 自部署接入指南](https://doc.fastgpt.io/zh-CN/self-host/custom-models/oxr)，补充自定义 PDF 解析配置与源文件名追踪，便于部署和定位解析问题。  ## ⚙️ 优化  1. **[工作流与 Agent]** 工作流与 Agent 辅助生成在本地无会话缓存时恢复服务端最近记录，恢复失败可重试；新建会话仅切换当前会话，保留历史及工作流版本归档。 2. **[工作流与 Agent]** 辅助生成按需准备沙盒，方案或画布基线实质变化时重新确认；沙盒丢失时根据已保存上下文重建。应用候选版本时优先关联可用系统密钥，无可用密钥的工具保持待激活。 3. **[工作流与 Agent]** 工作流候选版本的已应用状态在卡片和顶部提示间同步，未过期版本可再次应用；Agent 配置在实际应用完成后提示成功。 4. **[工作流与 Agent]** 改善模型原生工具调用流兼容性，处理并行参数混淆、名称重复或迟到、`functions.` 前缀及空参数；以工具调用状态结束但没有有效工具时明确返回错误。 5. **[工作流与 Agent]** 统一节点尺寸与画布布局，问题分类的默认文案随界面语言展示；提示词编辑器补充输入指引及系统、自定义变量选择器。 6. **[知识库与文件]** API 文件库同步先完成目录拉取与差量校验，再批量更新层级、名称和权限继承；远端请求失败时中止本轮同步，避免误删。服务地址兼容历史裸域名并默认补全 `https://`。 7. **[知识库与文件]** 启用同义词能力时，检索结果与引用可携带命中的标准词映射，重排兼顾原文与同义词标准化文本，保留较高分且不改写原文；未启用时保持原有行为。 8. **[知识库与文件]** 统一 AI 对话、Agent、工具与外部渠道的附件分类，区分文档、未知文件和图片、音视频，兼容旧文件类型；明确文档进入解析链路，未知文件仍可由文档读取工具尝试读取。无后缀链接支持从响应头及有限内容探测，复用文件名与请求内缓存。 9. **[知识库与文件]** 统一简易应用、Agent 与工作流的标签过滤交互，优化变量选择、启用状态和弹窗滚动；切换知识库清除无效自定义标签条件，手动输入关闭时保留已配置条件；工作流标签过滤补充结构示例与悬停说明。 10. **[知识库与文件]** `listV2` 可通过 `withFileCount` 按需获取全部层级的非目录文件数量；应用、技能与知识库支持按 ID 局部刷新，权限调整后保留分页及滚动位置。通用分页收到非数组列表时保留原有效数据并结束加载。 11. **[知识库与文件]** Agent 检索日志补充重排前候选快照，与知识库搜索节点统一使用 `retrievalResults`；仅在实际重排且 `RETRIEVAL_RESULTS_LIMIT > 0` 时记录，默认不记录。 12. **[对话体验]** 重试和编辑在删除成功后优先裁剪本地历史，减少额外历史请求；缺页时保留刷新，并丢弃裁剪前的过期分页响应。 13. **[对话体验]** 统一对话历史、资源选择器、发布历史及训练错误列表的初次加载与滚动追加状态，加载更多时保留已有内容，减少遮罩与嵌套滚动；切换查询或刷新后丢弃旧分页响应，避免旧数据混入当前列表。 14. **[对话体验]** 优化 Mermaid 的流式渲染、换行标签、缩放、拖拽与触控预览。 15. **[对话体验]** 简化加载动画与沙盒状态文案，改善思考过程、工具和技能详情的折叠、滚动与标题吸顶；错误标题、运行中节点与模型名称过长时省略显示，悬停可查看完整内容。 16. **[对话体验]** 支持通过 `/chat?pane=aa&tab=ta` 定位团队应用页签，刷新后保留选择。 17. **[对话体验]** 统一资源卡片间距，简介截断时可悬停查看完整内容。 18. **[工具与渠道]** MCP 工具失败及 HTTP 非 2xx 响应保留明确错误详情，便于排查限流与服务错误；MCP 收到 HTTP 429 时不再自动回退 SSE，避免重复连接。 19. **[团队与权限]** 初始化接口按登录状态返回公开配置，模型渠道和沙盒连接信息按需获取，账号切换后刷新缓存；成员名与登录用户名明确区分，强制填写时清除默认占位值，技能控制台补齐关联应用标签。 20. **[系统与运维]** 优化商业版应用与知识库管理列表查询，按 ID 倒序分页；知识库分块数统计仅查询当前页，并按团队与知识库配对匹配，减少全量扫描。 21. **[系统与运维]** 系统升级任务提示具体前置任务及对应语言文案，便于按依赖顺序执行或重试。 22. **[系统与运维]** 模型请求、请求记录与文件加载日志清理 base64 和不必要的调用栈，取消、探活中断及预期网络或用户配置错误按较低级别记录，减少无效告警。  ## 🐛 修复  1. **[系统与运维]** 修复 SeekDB 容器重启或重建后数据丢失的问题。 2. **[系统与运维]** 修复 Pro API 流式或无 Content-Type 响应被 NextAPI 默认 JSON 包裹破坏的问题，等待流结束并保留微信回调等原始响应。 3. **[系统与运维]** 修复 `InvokeProcessor` session getter 自递归导致调用栈溢出的问题。 4. **[系统与运维]** 修复沙盒通过命令编辑文件时将特殊字符当作正则、无法替换多行文本的问题，按原文匹配替换并保留符号链接及文件权限；文件缺失、无读取权限或未完整读取时明确报错，写入中断时保留原文件。 5. **[团队与权限]** 应用测试对话和工作流调试执行传入节点前必须校验编辑权限；Agent 调用系统工具使用实际运行成员身份，重新签发登录会话保留 root 身份。 6. **[团队与权限]** 修复重复启用文件级权限清空独立授权、父目录所有者权限错误继承及目录权限未同步到内部文件的问题，保留独立权限分支并按各知识库开关同步后代授权。 7. **[团队与权限]** 修复历史成员名为 `null` 或缺失时，登录、团队列表、成员列表及搜索响应校验失败的问题，统一回落为 `Member`，已有空字符串及名称保持原值。 8. **[团队与权限]** 修复复制或转换新建 Agent 绕过创建上限的问题，统一 License、创建额度与账号资源用量统计。 9. **[团队与权限]** 修复批量创建成员及多团队登录补建个人团队时的事务与并发异常，避免重复创建和权限读取失败。 10. **[团队与权限]** 修复资源移动到根目录时空父级 ID 导致 BSON 转换或权限更新异常的问题，并修复技能卡片无法拖入文件夹的问题；批量选择时关闭拖拽以避免操作冲突。 11. **[团队与权限]** 修复邀请链接进入团队时，已加入成员无法自动切换到目标团队、成功提示在刷新后丢失的问题；切换失败时保留已接受的邀请结果并提示。 12. **[团队与权限]** 修复商业版成员部门路径顺序或重复拼接异常，仅对同步模式下已禁用成员显示恢复操作。 13. **[知识库与文件]** 修复 PG 与 OpenGauss 向量检索的团队隔离问题，以及商业版图片解析和索引加载可引用其他知识库内部图片路径的问题。 14. **[知识库与文件]** 修复网站同步异常或提前截断时误删历史页面的问题；爬取因积分不足、模型配置错误、达到页数上限或页面失败而未完整结束时，跳过过期页面清理并记录结果。 15. **[知识库与文件]** 修复 CSV 导入后重解析丢失逐行问答、自定义索引和元数据的问题，导出不再将系统索引当作自定义索引，文件级权限开启时仅导出可读内容。 16. **[知识库与文件]** 修复多库检索因某个知识库缺少指定标签而整体排除该库的问题，否定和空值条件按缺少标签的语义匹配。 17. **[知识库与文件]** 修复 PPTX 备注与幻灯片顺序错乱、XLSX 系统日期格式丢失世纪信息的问题；Doc2x 表格正确转义单元格竖线并保留 LaTeX 语义。 18. **[知识库与文件]** 修复大体量 CSV、OFD 与 PDF 处理因展开参数过多而失败，以及 Markdown 超长表头中断整份文档导入的问题。 19. **[知识库与文件]** 修复带查询参数的文件链接、SVG XML 声明与 UTF-16 文本的类型和编码识别异常，统一上传大小及数量限制的前后端口径；取消 S3 分片上传时及时中止请求，避免无效异常。 20. **[知识库与文件]** 外部 PDF 解析服务返回加密或损坏错误时，提示解密或重新上传。 21. **[知识库与文件]** 文件下载和日志导出失败时提取明确错误消息，避免展示整段 JSON 或重复弹错。 22. **[知识库与文件]** 修复解析任务积分耗尽后反复报错的问题，等待后续团队余额检查暂停处理。 23. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复子工作流或工作流工具未捕获错误无法传到父节点，以及直接执行 MCP、HTTP、系统工具失败后不显示聊天错误卡片的问题；已捕获错误不向外提升，模型工具错误继续交给模型处理。 24. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复商业版工作流辅助生成无法读取上传附件的问题，恢复文档解析及沙盒文件访问；私有附件按当前应用、成员和会话校验归属。沙盒文本读取工具明确仅支持 UTF-8 纯文本，二进制文档需先解析或转换。 25. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复空说明表单提交时误报 dataId 重复的问题，兼容历史 `null` 及字段不完整的交互记录，保留有效交互的续跑信息。 26. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复节点再次执行时保留旧输出的问题，本次未返回的输出清空，避免循环或交互恢复读到旧值；成功后才补齐必填输出默认值。 27. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复判断器对数组及数字、布尔引用的包含判断，并保留正则中成对斜杠和转义语义，非法正则按错误处理。 28. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复模型引用提取与替换不一致的问题，兼容旧名称、模型 ID、重复输入和自定义选择器，避免误识别工具的同名业务参数。 29. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复代码助手生成的输入输出名称与实际参数 key 不一致、单步调试上传文件无法被解析节点识别的问题，保留已有输出标识及引用关系。 30. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复多变量引用删除按钮、拖拽时下拉弹层与侧边面板首次引导的交互问题，打开面板时结束引导，避免旧弹层遮挡画布。 31. **[工作流与 Agent]** 修复工具选择器进入 MCP、HTTP 工具集时目录路径报错的问题，按读取权限返回所在目录及工具集路径；合并系统工具列表加载入口，避免切换目录、标签或搜索时重复请求。 32. **[工具与渠道]** 修复 MCP 循环引用 Schema 导致响应序列化失败的问题；不规范的外部 JSON-RPC 响应展示明确的格式错误提示。 33. **[工具与渠道]** 修复未知 MCP SSE 会话一直等待的问题，未知会话返回 HTTP 404；无效应用访问 Key 返回携带请求 ID 的协议错误。 34. **[工具与渠道]** 修复模型工具参数被额外过滤或校验阻断、工具结果被历史与 SSE 再次截断的问题，保留原始参数和模型实际消费的完整结果，已配置固定参数优先。 35. **[工具与渠道]** 修复飞书未提供话题 ID 时会话标识异常的问题，无话题消息按群聊标识归入会话。 36. **[工具与渠道]** 工具版本选择器展示实际版本号，长 MCP 名称不再撑宽选择器。 37. **[工具与渠道]** 修正 OpenAPI 必填 ObjectId 标记与最小请求示例，避免调用方误判必填参数。 38. **[对话体验]** 修复流结束、取消及异常退出时续传状态清理不完整、跨会话控制器相互影响和跨标签页反复写入本地存储的问题。 39. **[对话体验]** 修复赞踩反馈保存失败后界面状态与服务端不一致的问题，保存成功后再更新并提示结果；防止重复提交及切换会话后的迟到响应串写。 40. **[对话体验]** 修复并发生成会话标题时重复请求或旧结果覆盖的问题，保留手动标题，并保持未设置的 `customTitle` 为未设置状态。 41. **[对话体验]** 修复表格复制携带 CSV 转义引号，以及不同换行符导致复制或导出后错行的问题。 42. **[对话体验]** 修复文本预览截断 emoji 等字符的代理对导致乱码的问题。 43. **[对话体验]** 修复时间越界导致日期进位的问题，失焦时限制时分秒范围，切换日期保留时间编辑。 44. **[对话体验]** 修复文件大小进位展示异常的问题，避免显示 `1024 KB` 等结果。 45. **[模型与计费]** 修复 Agent 请求失败时遗漏已产生的 Token 与积分、历史思考 Token 预估缺失，以及 `maxInputTokens=0` 误判为无上限的问题。 46. **[模型与计费]** 修复思考模型在工具续轮、长度续写和 Agent 多轮调用中丢失 `reasoning_content` 的问题，保留已有思考文本，无思考文本时也按模型能力回传空字段。 47. **[模型与计费]** 修复开票列表刷新后选中记录丢失或错位的问题；商业版开票支持银行订单，并校验全部订单归属、金额与开票状态，防止重复申请。 48. **[模型与计费]** 修复套餐编辑将未填写配额转成 0 的问题，留空沿用标准值，显式 0 保留。 49. **[模型与计费]** 修复定价页面月付、年付计费切换的选中背景。  ## 🛠️ 代码优化  1. **[工作流与 Agent]** 抽取共享 `workflow-core` 与 `workflow-cli`，统一节点、引用、条件分支和校验诊断；拆分资源授权、快照加载与运行时执行职责，减少重复校验。 2. **[工作流与 Agent]** 统一 Agent Ask 与辅助生成预览的结构化回复及持久化，拆分技能调试业务与 HTTP 适配，缓存辅助生成节点模板，减少重复写入。 3. **[对话体验]** 抽取统一聊天反馈上下文，集中管理赞踩、标注、自定义反馈及已读操作；按页面显式启用反馈类型，减少消息、控制器和弹窗间的逐层传参。 4. **[工具与渠道]** 补充企业微信 WebSocket 发布渠道的数据模型、凭据配置与连接状态管理。 5. **[系统与运维]** 区分错误分支数据、原始异常和展示详情，统一循环、并行与子工作流错误统计；用户配置错误保留本地诊断，跳过 OTel 日志导出，避免与系统故障混淆。修复不同 Next.js 模块实例间链路追踪状态不共享的问题。 6. **[系统与运维]** 运行镜像升级 Node.js 至 24.22.1，并更新 Hono、Vite、Multer、Nodemailer、Undici、Zod、MCP SDK、KaTeX 等依赖；升级 OpenSandbox SDK 与部署组件，清理未使用的 Deep RAG 路径及管理台依赖。 ### 2026-11-03T01:07:23Z [**cloudflare/pingora**](https://github.com/cloudflare/pingora)
```

```
## [0.9.1](https://github.com/cloudflare/pingora/compare/0.9.0...0.9.1) - 2026-11-02   **🐛 Bug Fixes**  * Fix a panic on every accepted and outgoing connection with tokio 1.54 and later, which rejects zero-capacity buffered readers (#1059). * Fix the docs.rs build of the `pingora` crate, which failed for 0.9.0 (#1018). * Fix a panic in `pingora-limits` `Estimator::incr` when a counter overflows in builds with overflow checks enabled (#1041).  **🔒 Security**  * Require zstd 0.14.3, and through it zstd-safe 8.2.0 and zstd-sys 2.1.2, so dependency resolution cannot select zstd crates affected by RUSTSEC-2026-0391, RUSTSEC-2026-0395 through RUSTSEC-2026-0406, or RUSTSEC-2026-0410.  **⚙️ Miscellaneous Tasks**  * Ignore RustSec advisories for rustls 0.21, webpki-roots 0.26, rustls-webpki 0.101 and crypto-bigint 0.5.5, which reach the lockfile only through optional transitive dependencies and have no compatible fixed release. * Update HTTP/2 rejection tests for h2 releases that reject conflicting Content-Length headers in the codec. 
```

### 2026-11-03T12:23:16Z [**open-webui/open-webui**](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui)

```
 ### Added  - 📑 **Tabbed workspace.** The Tabs button at the top of a chat now opens a workspace for files, multiple terminal sessions, artifacts, previews, voice calls and comments. Add tabs from the + menu, drag to reorder them, and create and save new files directly into the selected terminal. Keep the conversation and its working files together while coding, analyzing data or reviewing generated documents. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2d7cf5ec99c3fa5c7ee48b91823fb8021b846cf3), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5b58b1ebc03024fca1efd498aeabe4d9880dc3aa), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a06aa230f75cc2d3512d1bca197a98f3c35da96f), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/cdc6feecd3769aac41844d81bd775dfb69b44912) - 🖥️ **Open Terminal Desktop tab for computer use.** Open the Desktop tab from the + menu to view and control the desktop provided by your connected Open Terminal, with mouse, keyboard, clipboard and audio support where available. With a model that can call its desktop tools, you can follow computer-use workflows in the same desktop and step in yourself: operate graphical apps, inspect generated documents or handle a step that needs manual input. Requires an Open Terminal connection with desktop support enabled. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/b51e68fbb3e641e7f9027f5164fcf6b0e111cf27), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3bd0cdd0f3cc075420540462f2bf246c73236495), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/03401a6e331d25425533933da85d3e5496deca3f), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/6dd3cc216f63085d2a1105117c65064c3e0ca3b6) - 🌐 **Browser tab for browser use and web app testing.** Open websites and local development servers beside your chat, with navigation controls, multiple tabs and a mobile viewport. On a compatible Open Terminal connection, Chrome mode gives you and the model a shared browser session for browser automation: navigate sites, interact with forms and test the web apps the model builds. You can watch the work and interact with the page yourself; Proxy mode remains available for previews. Requires browser support on the selected terminal, and Chrome availability for browser-use tools. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fbe69b01fd58a374211d348a28eb5988b11d41de), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3bd0cdd0f3cc075420540462f2bf246c73236495), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9260e312fb4238b504652eff0d8ca45445ca0821) - ↔️ **Full-width tabs.** On larger screens, expand the tabbed workspace to fill the chat area, with the main conversation kept as its own tab. Switch between the chat, code, browser and desktop with more room to work, or return to the side-by-side layout; your layout choice is remembered. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/09030d5dd2def4324d98c25f92cf54b1ec1844f1), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1ce8616ab9587ca717015d5af9cb26ee4296311f) - 💻 **Open WebUI Desktop 0.1.x.** The separately installed app for macOS, Windows and Linux now focuses on setting up and running Open WebUI on your own computer, with optional llama.cpp local models. The 0.1.0–0.1.4 updates bring a slimmer interface, searchable settings, startup logs, optional automatic Desktop updates, start-to-tray controls and image copying and saving. They also improve screen sharing, knowledge folder uploads, Linux graphics and shortcuts, and NVIDIA/AMD local-model setup and updates. Use it for a managed local installation and local AI workflows. [Desktop changelog](https://github.com/open-webui/desktop/blob/v0.1.4/CHANGELOG.md), [Download v0.1.4](https://github.com/open-webui/desktop/releases/tag/v0.1.4) - 🌿 **Git in the side panel.** When the selected terminal's folder is a Git repository, a Git panel in the tabbed side panel now lets you review diffs, stage, discard and commit changes, manage branches and worktrees, push and pull, browse history and work with GitHub pull requests, and open files can show their changes with Show diff. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/58926cfe7b98aab83c2544714d26cdbcc90722a3), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3e24a08f8ec8472dcbbc46e7e39ab35aaa4a6f4f), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/30f0779d12072f98b2c3ce1b08819c72ab9c66d8), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/295b0778938cb9a133712b1bda4a18df92406375), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/250108302e6d73406c643a51eb5d9f80d39ddc03) - ⚡ **Live terminal updates.** On terminals that support it, the Git panel, the list of open ports and the running processes and their output now update as soon as something changes instead of every few seconds. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c4c10aa2559e10883797e3dd6702e16dc36eff19), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/70c5e38fc7ce3864426b484c07ee201cba2b9d07) - 💭 **Side chats.** Typing "/side" or "/btw" in a chat, or choosing Side chat in the side panel, now opens a temporary side conversation using the chat so far as context. Ask a quick question or explore an alternative without adding the detour to the main conversation; the side chat disappears when you close its tab. Available to administrators and users given the new Allow Side Chat permission, which is off by default. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/321701c1af09d58f9ed6b67a22416ab8ddcfa165), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/151f7a84d1ca6949234671cd86d697abbce76444), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/27963030eccda5b8b27905ec0a35d5d9f09788e6) - 💬 **Comment threads.** Administrators can now turn on Comments in Admin Settings > General or with "ENABLE_COMMENTS", which lets users leave comments with replies, reactions and mentions on the chats, notes, models, knowledge bases, prompts, skills and tools they can open, including on a selected passage of a chat message, which then stays highlighted in the reply, or of a note page, shown in a Comments tab in the chat's side panel, which "/comments" opens, and with comment counts in the workspace lists. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9d1b4ef362f103f2dda05760a44c96df82c064fd), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/91e8deee74c627fee29c4c81833766040334170f), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/63312766f8de94bc8a054d7923f2f83410b841a2), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/04d78a483de671dbe789082b79638f9e44078819) - 🔔 **Notification inbox.** A new Inbox in the sidebar collects comments on your items, replies to your comments, mentions in comments and channels, replies in channels, failed automation runs and upcoming calendar events with an unread count, and administrators can turn it off in Admin Settings > General or with "ENABLE_NOTIFICATIONS". [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3814b7b2f152c671fd3789e5a0099313d5316467), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/1a870d9481630861fe6d63de835d983df50b72c3), [#19539](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/19539) - 🧩 **MCP Apps in chat.** Tools from MCP servers that come with an interactive app now show that app right in the chat after they run, in a sandboxed frame that can only reach the sites the server declares, and every tool call the app wants to make waits for you to confirm it. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0e0ba872a4d3e70c4e19e5c62b05fd7187b917f3), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4ab90a6c1ff9526640e73ad37e67e88e693e2172), [#21040](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/21040), [#20915](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/20915) - ✨ **Generate title from top bar.** The rename field at the top of an open chat now has the same sparkle button as the sidebar, which generates a title from the conversation and saves it right away. [#33382](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33382) - 📐 **Side panels on tablets.** On screens from 768 pixels wide, such as tablets and narrow windows, channel threads and the pages list and chat in notes now open beside the content instead of over it, picking a terminal opens its files beside the chat as well, and the search dialog shows its preview there by default and remembers whether you turned it on or off at any window size. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/cbeb8e3630cafd7ef6fd28b552bf7114729f21b9), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/c5981aaea6b25bb9b9601e62b9ff75da33aaaf4e) - 📊 **More PowerPoint charts.** The PowerPoint preview now draws pie, doughnut and horizontal bar charts as well as values below zero, and shows "Chart preview unavailable" for charts it cannot draw. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/09fbbd6d69293084d9e09cbb7d3b93e5d305ec5c), [#32884](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/32884) - 🧊 **3D model previews.** GLB, glTF, STL, OBJ and PLY files in a terminal's file browser and in files shown in chat replies now open in a 3D viewer that you can rotate and zoom. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fe0d22cc24ddc2421b4e36a66a174188cc61d265) - 🔘 **Switch tools on and off.** Tools in Workspace > Tools now have an on and off switch for everyone who can edit them, and a tool that is switched off can no longer be picked or run in any chat, including chats where it was already selected. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/9d1b4ef362f103f2dda05760a44c96df82c064fd) - 🛠️ **Tool creation from chat.** Models with native function calling can now create a private Python tool in your workspace when you ask them to, for administrators and users allowed to create workspace tools, and it can be turned off per model under Tool Creation in the model editor's builtin tools. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/91241306e2541e984591435d8c25e9ab1442db6b) - 🤝 **Multi-model sub-agents.** The model editor now lets you choose other models for a model to delegate work to, enabling multi-agent workflows such as assigning research, coding and review to different models. The parent model chooses among the configured models the user can access; previously, sub-agents always used the same model as the parent. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/789a0f402abe05e028bc8e0a8824d58b94e26d8f) - 📏 **Knowledge base file limits.** Administrators can now cap how many files a knowledge base holds, how large each of its files may be and how many files go into it in one upload, for all knowledge bases in Admin Settings > Documents or with "KNOWLEDGE_MAX_FILE_COUNT", "KNOWLEDGE_MAX_FILE_SIZE" and "KNOWLEDGE_MAX_UPLOAD_COUNT", and a knowledge base's owner can set its own file count and size limits under Advanced when creating it or from Settings in its menu, while the global limits always apply. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/7618df3f53a8db2ad5352a789c88673a736b07a4), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/5c4fb61d862b011cf9efda6b2289de637ba8d6bb), [#33318](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33318), [#24845](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/24845) - 📄 **File sizes in previews.** Opened terminal files now show their size in the file toolbar, so you can check a document or generated output without leaving its preview. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2e55c365645e538c54b0d31b7bc8a977f6bb8b85) - 🗂️ **Sort files by date added.** The terminal file browser can now sort files by when they were added, next to name, size and date modified, on terminals that report creation times. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/8c308e4e88c48924209f0018d73beb82a4ba2d94) - ↕️ **Move blocks in notes.** Every paragraph, heading and other block in a note now has a handle beside it that you can drag to move the block, or click to move it up or down. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3bb962f2bbd82ebe8ccad6f64304956aee2f4f97) - 📋 **Copy code in notes.** Code blocks in notes now have a Copy button and use clearer syntax colors in light mode. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ab71357980dab80de23b3597a13a41557a11df99), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ee8b69e718aa7f0b962309e0137aa81f557093eb), [#33455](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33455) - ⏸️ **Approvals shown in sidebar.** Chats in the sidebar now show a "Waiting for approval" marker while a tool call in them waits for you to allow or deny it. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ddb43c83bac60553b002f148265db0900bc2b9f4), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2840f0e07b398b76db04978334e3232fe8b89c31) - 🏷️ **Role prompt variable.** System prompts, saved prompts and chat requests through the API can now use "{{USER_ROLE}}", which is replaced with the signed-in user's role, such as admin or user. [#33441](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33441), [#4765](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/4765) - ♿ **Accessibility improvements.** Screen reader users now hear a name for every window, form field, setting and icon button, including Admin Settings, Valves, filters and channel message actions, and keyboard users can answer tool approvals, dismiss notifications, open model details in Admin Analytics, sort Analytics and group member tables, resize the sidebar, open notebook cells for editing, use multi-select lists in Valves, pick a status emoji, choose tag and voice suggestions, open the sources of a web search, use the menus in the Search window, stay inside the full-size image viewer and side drawers until they close them and reach every event on the calendar. Closing a window opened from a menu now returns focus to the menu button, Tab cancels shortcut recording, task-list checkboxes are named by their text, and unselected Analytics tabs have better contrast in Accessibility Mode. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a671146b9172407d11240b8130eda9e92dfb2225), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0171427a10f168da0d5b733fa5d0786c4d63b851), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/51cef9e26f17ad5baf1f6d51f72d87651d7e98e4), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/0a18dfc1841f99b143ae697eff0e51a24057d4fd) - 🔄 **General improvements.** Various improvements were implemented across the application to enhance performance, stability, and security. - 🌐 **Translation updates.** Translations for Portuguese (Brazil), Latvian, Azerbaijani, Basque, Bengali, Bosnian, Bulgarian, Cebuano, Croatian, Czech, Danish, Dutch, Estonian, Faroese, Filipino, Galician, German, Greek, Hebrew, Hungarian, Indonesian, Irish, Japanese, Korean, Lithuanian, Malay, Norwegian Bokmål, Persian, Polish, Punjabi, Romanian, Russian, Serbian, Slovak, Slovenian, Spanish (Spain), Tamil, Turkish, Turkmen, Ukrainian and Vietnamese were enhanced and expanded.  ### Fixed  - 🛡️ **Security Advisory**: This release contains **security fixes**. We **strongly recommend updating** all deployments **as soon as possible** to have the latest security fixes in place. These fixes are **not listed in this changelog**; we will publish the security advisories for them immediately following CVE assignment. [Advisories](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/security) - 🔃 **New chats stay in sync.** A chat started from the home page keeps syncing with the server again after it is created, so files removed from the chat's Controls no longer come back after a reload and the owner of a chat shared with Allow replies gets Regenerate on other people's replies again. [#33377](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33377), [#33376](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33376) - 📂 **Temporary Chat on folder pages.** Turning Temporary Chat on or off on a folder's page, with the button at the top or with "/temporary", now keeps you on the folder page, so the next chat is still saved in the folder and gets its system prompt, default model and knowledge. [#33456](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33456), [#33448](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33448) - 🧵 **Thread reply focus.** Choosing Reply in Thread on a channel message now puts the cursor in the thread's reply box, also when the thread panel is already open. [#33467](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33467), [#33466](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33466) - 🪟 **Thread message actions visible.** Hovering the top message in a channel thread now shows its whole actions toolbar instead of cutting off its top half at the edge of the panel. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/08f197ad8d87b4f71be311888541c601942e96b9), [#33472](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33472) - 📞 **Interrupting realtime calls.** Interrupting the model while it speaks in a realtime voice call no longer ends the whole call when the reported playback position does not match the audio. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/e5a2e6438e6f7455c7f0dcc304cd1b08329afe8b), [#33550](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33550) - 📶 **Temporary chats after reconnecting.** A reply that is still being written in a temporary chat now keeps arriving after the connection drops and comes back, and a reply that could not finish shows "Connection lost. Please retry." instead of loading forever. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/321701c1af09d58f9ed6b67a22416ab8ddcfa165) - 🏠 **New chat initialization.** Opening or reloading the home page or a folder now selects the model and applies chat URL parameters again, instead of leaving the model empty and ignoring the link’s settings. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/082df44d92178b6895153774bf6656c538cbce40) - ✍️ **Restored chat drafts.** Saved draft text now returns to the message input after the new-chat editor mounts, instead of disappearing during initialization. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/4ea29c0d6198e44a84aae01475b003c8bc192e5b) - 🧩 **Nested code previews.** Preview now opens code blocks inside lists, quotes and details blocks, so embedded examples and generated artifacts can be inspected directly. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/aec5796a535beaf3730e8c8a8b0426a94b3b1b44) - 🖼️ **Image drops from photo apps.** Images dropped into chats and channels are now recognized from their contents when the source omits the file type, including photos dragged from macOS Photos, instead of being treated as generic files. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/563d621854e7a465a7713a811e996729a3e23499) - 📏 **Live context usage estimates.** The context indicator now accounts for output arriving after the latest reported token usage and avoids counting assistant text twice when it also appears in structured output, making it easier to track context-window usage during long agent runs. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/8e61cc02106e4f698494c7813fa6602fd46f5f87) - 🔌 **Responses-backed API streaming.** Clients using Open WebUI’s Chat Completions API now receive the expected streaming format even when the upstream model uses the Responses API, including text, reasoning, tool calls, token usage and completion reasons. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/e789513ea62eb0c56f36d7c48996f084931f1c49) - 😀 **Channel reaction names.** Hovering a reaction on a channel message now shows who reacted when those names are available, instead of showing only the count. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/ea1c60fbdf3cfc08ddf4128ab33007380228e86c) - 📊 **Analytics activity scrolling.** The activity chart now tracks the element actually being scrolled, keeping its visible rows in sync when browsing longer activity lists. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f0e7d718caaba875621bf686c13c9c021d28643d) - 🔑 **Saved OAuth provider settings.** OAuth providers now load their saved connection settings when Open WebUI starts. Admin Settings also explains that changes to provider connections require restarting every Open WebUI instance. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/bab66b2c2ff0e48b2a07a278d73e490b6577297c)  ### Changed  - ⚠️ **Database Migrations**: This release includes database schema changes; we strongly recommend backing up your database and all associated data before upgrading in production environments. If you are running a multi-worker, multi-server, or load-balanced deployment, all instances must be updated simultaneously, rolling updates are not supported and will cause application failures due to schema incompatibility. - 🎛️ **Controls in the model menu.** The chat files, valves, system prompt and advanced parameters moved from the Controls panel at the top of the chat to Controls in the model controls menu next to the message input, and the button at the top now opens the tabbed side panel. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/2d7cf5ec99c3fa5c7ee48b91823fb8021b846cf3), [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/950da84bdb57423d9d2ddd6d29659d56174d8c22) - 🗺️ **Overview in the chat menu.** The chat Overview, the map of all messages and their branches, now opens as a full-screen window from Overview in the chat's menu instead of as a tab in Chat Controls. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/fe0d22cc24ddc2421b4e36a66a174188cc61d265) - 🔁 **Higher tool-call limit.** A single response with native function calling can now run up to 1024 rounds of tool calls before it stops with "Tool-call limit reached", up from 256, and setting "CHAT_RESPONSE_MAX_TOOL_CALL_ITERATIONS" to 256 keeps the old limit. [#33384](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33384) - 🔓 **No sub-agent limits by default.** The sub-agent limits in Admin Settings and through "SUBAGENTS_MAX_CONCURRENT", "SUBAGENTS_MAX_ASYNC", "SUBAGENTS_MAX_ITERATIONS" and "SUBAGENTS_MAX_OUTPUT" now default to no limit, and leaving one empty means no limit, where they defaulted to 20 at once, 20 in the background, 30 tool loops each and 30000 characters of output. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/f0cc1bb86e52ec629993f5cd26f5f001bd5b72a6) - 🧠 **Context compaction and prompt caching.** When the same model handles the conversation and its compaction summary, eligible requests now reuse the conversation’s prompt structure so the provider can use its prompt cache. This can reduce the cost of summarizing long conversations and agent workflows; other requests retain the separate summary path. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/a8b478ed7b8d22fe5b8f96be3c4cfd08cd30affb) - 💻 **Desktop 0.1.x upgrade changes.** Open WebUI Desktop no longer switches between remote servers, bundles Open Terminal, or provides the old Spotlight and global voice overlays. Existing remote-connection users should review [Open WebUI Tether](https://github.com/open-webui/tether). The Open Terminal Desktop and Browser tabs described above use a separately configured Open Terminal connection. [Desktop 0.1.0 release notes](https://github.com/open-webui/desktop/releases/tag/v0.1.0) - 📭 **File processing alerts removed.** Personal notifications in Settings > Notifications no longer offer "Retrieval Content process failed", so notification webhooks no longer receive an alert when a file fails to process. [Commit](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/commit/3814b7b2f152c671fd3789e5a0099313d5316467) 
```

### 2026-11-02T18:32:27Z [**gitbutlerapp/gitbutler**](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler)

```
##Fixes  - Fixes commits and amendments disturbing staged files or hiding later file edits - Fixes problems caused by missing target-branch tracking: older projects failing to open, missing pull request details and skipped remote updates - Fixes the app not refreshing after external worktree changes and showing errors for worktrees removed outside the app - Fixes merged pull requests disappearing from worktree branches after updating the workspace - Fixes undo leaving newly created branches behind or failing to restore deleted branches after Git garbage collection - GitLab: Fixes merge request listing on older instances and authentication with server URLs ending in `/api/v4/` - Bitbucket: Fixes pull request creation and browser links for remotes using `ssh.bitbucket.org` - Creating an independent branch from the target now checks it out without unnecessarily switching to the workspace - Fixes unstacking failures with linked worktrees and branches disappearing from the native app when unstacked  ## CLI  - `but agent setup` now supports Amp; `but agent uninstall` removes GitButler skills and instructions from coding agents - Claude desktop agents can now enable Auto-fix for pull requests created with `but pr new` in workspace mode - `but worktree new --above` now accepts branch names, IDs and Git revisions; worktree removal and archive commands also accept checkout paths - `but commit -b` can now create a branch and commit from a detached linked worktree - Running `but` inside an archived linked worktree now unarchives it automatically - `but branch delete` now handles branches outside the workspace and protects branches checked out in linked worktrees - `but resolve` gains clearer recovery hints, stdin input with `apply -F -` and checks for leftover conflict markers; resolved branches now return success - `but diff` now accepts committed hunk IDs and reports file statuses correctly in JSON output - `but branch show --ai` now uses the configured AI model - `but pull` avoids unnecessary worktree updates, and `--update worktrees` leaves the main checkout unchanged - `but config target origin/main` now creates the local tracking branch when needed - `but open` is removed; use `but gui` or `but .`. Retired `but branch new --anchor` now points to `--above` in its error hint  ## Experimental  Highlights from the native app, available in the settings under "experimental":  - Adds integrated terminals for each checkout, with agent status indicators and drag-and-drop support - Adds a commit graph showing the workspace, linked worktrees and target history - Adds a TUI-style keyboard layout and modes for moving, committing and squashing changes; custom bindings are no longer available in the native app - Adds worktree creation, reordering and updates, plus configurable inactivity grouping (two days by default) - Adds in-app pull request editing, auto-merge on creation and clearer CI checks - Improved commit and diff editing, with inline rewording, single-file diffs and copying files or hunks as Git patches  ## New Contributors * @kunalgoesbyken made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/16485 * @RoooyHe made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/16705 * @definitelynotguru made their first contribution in https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/pull/16745  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/compare/release/0.23.1...release/0.23.2  ## Downloads  Download bins from https://gitbutler.com/ 
```
