## Spotube V6 Beta is here 🎉🚀  ### 🔥Highlights  - Migration from Flutter to Compose Multiplatform, [learn why we did it](https://spotube.cc/blog/compose-multiplatform-migration/) - UI redesign, introducing [Millennium](https://github.com/team-spotube/millennium/) UI. 2000s look and feel with 2026's modernity - Jam session integration, play music with friends and family - Full Android Auto integration. Browse, Library, Search all are there now, not just queue - Migration from hetu_script to Kotlin for plugin development. Powered by [Zipline by Cashapp](https://github.com/cashapp/zipline/) - More extensibility, plugin support for Lyrics provider and Scrobbling providers - Native Android APK, with reduced apk size, performance improvements and more native android integrations. - Relicensed from BSD-4-Clause to AGPL-3.0-or-later for a more Open Source friendly licensing. And for preventing distribution of closed source and maliciously modified Spotube.  ### 💫 Features  - Migration to Compose Multiplatform - Add ChipTab component and refactor SearchScreen to use it - Add AutocompleteTextField component and integrate it into SearchScreen - Enhance AutocompleteTextField with focus management and dropdown behavior - Enhance AppSidebar with improved button interactions and visual feedback - Implement endless playback recommendations in DeviceAudioPlayerQueue - Add GhostIconButton component and enhance button interactions in TrackList - Replace IconButton with GhostIconButton in AppExpandedPlayer and AppLargePlayer for improved visual consistency - Replace IconButton with GhostIconButton in PlayerQueueContent and PlayableCard for visual consistency - Enhance UI components with LocalBaseUITheme for consistent styling and improved interactions - Enhance button styles and interactions in Buttons and Theme components for improved UI consistency - Simplify highlight implementation in UI components for improved readability and maintainability - Add genre selection and tabs to HomeScreen for improved content navigation - Implement artist overview screen with loading, error handling, and pagination for albums, related artists, and featured playlists - Add genre and featured browsing capabilities to MediaBrowseHelper - Implement playlist creation and editing functionality with UI components - Add 'Add to Playlist' functionality with UI components and dialogs - Add Toggle component and update UI to use it in place of Switch - Implement settings section cards for updates, desktop, language, caching, and plugins - Update icons in settings sections to use Iconsax library for improved UI consistency - Replace FeatherIcons with Iconsax in PluginCard and PluginPermissionsDialog for improved UI consistency - Refactor PluginPermissionsDialog to use custom UI components for improved consistency and readability - Enhance UI with shared element transitions and improve state management in album and playlist screens - Integrate Uniffi for audio metadata reading and writing with Lofty library - Replace FeatherIcons with Iconsax in window controls and update sidebar with better design - Implement haze effect and UI enhancements in AppLargePlayer and AppShell - Enhance haze effect integration in AppShell and AppSidebar for improved UI experience - Add adaptive dialog bottom sheet component and enhance plugin installation UI - Enhance AdaptiveDialogBottomSheet with dismiss icon and improve plugin screen layout - Implement CollectionView component and refactor Album, Playlist, SavedTracks screens for improved UI consistency - Add current time management in AudioPlayer for improved playback control - Simplify playback control logic in AudioPlayer by removing redundant state checks - Introduce AudioPlayerInterface and refactor AudioPlayer implementations for better abstraction - Update skipToNext and skipToPrevious methods in AudioPlayer for improved media item management - Refactor LyricsViewModel to use AudioPlayerInterface for improved abstraction - Add Discord Rich Presence integration for enhanced media playback visibility - Implement System Tray integration for window management and media controls - Add hover effect to menu items in System Tray for improved user experience - Add SSH debug session setup on failure for improved troubleshooting - Implement blacklist functionality for tracks and artists with UI updates - **systemtray**: Self-manage tray lifecycle via Koin DI - **plugin_interfaces**: Update Maven publishing configuration and add GitHub Actions workflow - **plugin_discovery**: Implement GitHub plugin discovery and add logo support in UI - **plugin_discovery**: Implement GitHub plugin discovery and add logo support and information dialog in UI - **plugin_install**: Add plugin installation dialog and release fetching functionality - Update dependencies and configure compose hot reload settings - Improve NewPipe video rank matching - Integrate Nucleus framework for enhanced window management and webview support - Implement system tray functionality with Compose Native Tray integration - **mediacontrol**: Implement SystemMediaControlService for OS media integration - **devices**: Add device discovery and remote control features - **deeplinks**: Implement deep link handling for jam sessions - **remote-control**: Implement remote playback functionality and UI components - **remote-control**: Enhance queue management and player state synchronization - **remote-control**: Enhance remote playback functionality with new commands and UI integration - **webrtc**: Update ICE server configuration and enhance logging for peer connection events - **jam-session**: Add connection state management and update UI feedback for session status - **jam-session**: Enhance jam session functionality with improved playback synchronization and error handling - ### 2026-10-29T17:41:17Z [**ikatson/rqbit**](https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit)

```
rqbit 9.1.0 is mostly a stability and correctness release, with a few small features. Several fixes stop a remote peer from crashing rqbit: deeply nested bencode, an out-of-range ut_metadata request, and a panic in the incoming-connection listener. It also adds `socks5h://` proxies, fixes BEP 52 hybrid torrents that couldn't be added, and fixes a web UI bug that cut output folder names at `#`.  **Library users:** `PeerConnectionOptions` has a new `max_request_window` field. If you build that struct with every field listed instead of using `..Default::default()`, add the new field.  ## Features * Support `socks5h://` proxy URLs by @jpds in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/683 * Make the per-peer request window configurable by @melkir in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/687 * Stats: count connected peers that have the full torrent by @zond in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/649  ## Crash and security fixes * bencode: limit deserializer recursion depth (remote stack-overflow abort) by @tweedge in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/660 * Fix off-by-one in ut_metadata bounds check (remote panic) by @MarcelCoding in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/704 * Don't panic in listener accept by @ikatson in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/674  ## Fixes * Torrents: don't reject duplicate `.pad/<length>` filenames, so BEP 52 hybrid torrents can be added, by @tweedge in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/701 * Web UI: output folder no longer truncated at `#` when adding torrents, by @qnfm in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/710 * Docker: make the CA cert bundle world-readable, so the image runs as a non-root user, by @Allypost in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/712 * Route blocklist/allowlist downloads through the proxied client by @jpds in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/669 * Peers: detect capabilities from the handshake on incoming connections by @zond in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/651 * Peers: accept an extended handshake missing the `m` dictionary by @jpds in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/684 * Peers: read the handshake until it is complete by @ArshPunisher in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/699 * DHT: fix duplicate `request_one` call by @moshen in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/646 * DHT: keep the worker alive after a per-datagram recv error on Windows by @nickEatsBread in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/690 * LSD: cancel the announce task once the torrent is removed by @lvella in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/700 * Storage: write past 2 GiB where pwritev takes a 32-bit `off_t` by @zond in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/661 * Windows: mark files sparse regardless of the log level by @tvollstaedt in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/681 * Release the state lock before `update_only_files` touches the peer table by @zond in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/659 * Take a piece out of the download queue once we have it by @zond in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/655 * Only treat 40-character hex strings as info hashes by @melkir in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/688 * Streaming: wait for the initial check in `ManagedTorrent::stream` by @melkir in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/689 * Storage API: don't lose `is_type_id` through boxing and wrapping (#652), forward `pwrite_all_vectored` through `Box` (#654), and forward `on_piece_completed` through the storage middlewares (#653), all by @zond * Core: `CurrentPiece::piece_offset` is now the offset within the piece by @MemoDo-En in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/695 * Build: use `.PHONY` instead of `@PHONY` in Makefiles by @TUKAIL in https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/pull/676 * Dependencies updated (#716)  ## New Contributors @moshen, @zond, @tweedge, @jpds, @TUKAIL, @tvollstaedt, @melkir, @nickEatsBread, @ArshPunisher, @MarcelCoding, @Allypost, @qnfm, @lvella and @MemoDo-En made their first contributions. Thank you!  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ikatson/rqbit/compare/v9.0.1...v9.1.0 
```

### 2026-10-31T07:45:15Z [**libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu**](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu)

```
> 简体中文版本请展开页面底部的「简体中文」。  ## Read Before Upgrading  - **With TUN on, the TUN stack chosen in Clash settings now takes precedence over a Profile's `tun.stack`.** Previously a stack written by the subscription won. If you relied on it, choose the stack in settings instead (the default is gVisor). - **A Profile that sets a TUN stack the selected core rejects now fails to apply, with an error naming the value and the supported ones.** For example, `tun.stack: mixed` or `mips` under Clash Premium, which would otherwise make the core reject the whole config. This is checked even while TUN is off, because the core parses the key on load. Values Nyanpasu does not know are still passed to the core. - **The stack selector only lists the stacks the current core reads**, and is hidden for clash-rs and Meow, which ignore the key. If the saved stack is not supported by the core you switch to, gVisor is used and a warning is written to the log; the saved setting is kept. - **Service mode now requires nyanpasu-service 2.0.2.** An installed 2.0.1 service is upgraded once at startup. 2.0.2 fixes a stale pid record that made the service and the app fail on every start (#6033).  ## Highlights  - **Mips (Mihomo IP Stack)**, mihomo's new user-space TUN stack, can be selected under mihomo and mihomo-alpha, together with its TCP congestion controller (CUBIC, Reno, BBR, BBRv3, or the core default). The controller is remembered when you switch to another stack and back (#6091). - **Startup failures no longer crash the app.** A dialog names the file, the cause and the recovery steps, and can open the containing, backup or log folder. Corrupt local data and an unusable jobs journal are rebuilt instead of blocking startup.  ## [2.0.1] - 2026-10-31   ### ✨ Features  - **config:** Add the mips TUN stack and its congestion controller (#6098) by @greenhat616  in [#6098](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6098)  - **config:** Decide the TUN stack from each core's config features (#6097) by @greenhat616  in [#6097](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6097)  - **settings:** Offer the TUN stacks and mips options the core reads (#6099) by @greenhat616  in [#6099](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6099)  - **startup:** Report failures in a dialog instead of panicking (#6063) by @greenhat616  in [#6063](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6063)  - **windows:** Bundle the Windows App SDK bootstrapper with enableLoopback (#6043) by @greenhat616  in [#6043](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6043)   ### 🐛 Bug Fixes  - **jobs:** Rebuild an unusable jobs journal instead of failing to start (#6069) by @greenhat616  in [#6069](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6069)  - **service:** Require service 2.0.2 (#6045) by @greenhat616  in [#6045](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6045)  - **startup:** Handle environment failures before and around setup (#6064) by @greenhat616  in [#6064](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6064)  - **startup:** Rebuild corrupt local data instead of failing to start (#6062) by @greenhat616  in [#6062](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6062)  - Align update link with header menu (#6109) by @keiko233  in [#6109](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6109)  - Prevent logo animation edge artifacts (#6041) by @keiko233  in [#6041](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6041)  - Prevent page title artifacts while scrolling (#6040) by @keiko233  in [#6040](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6040)  - Prevent sparkline stretching on new peaks (#6039) by @keiko233  in [#6039](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6039)   ### 📚 Documentation  - **config:** Design core feature queries and the TUN mips stack (#6096) by @greenhat616  in [#6096](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6096)  - **design:** Add domain error design notes (#6083) by @greenhat616  in [#6083](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6083)  - Plan startup failure handling without setup panics (#6060) by @greenhat616  in [#6060](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6060)   ### 🔨 Refactor  - **setup:** Report startup failures as domain errors (#6061) by @greenhat616  in [#6061](https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/pull/6061)  -----------------    **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/libnyanpasu/clash-nyanpasu/compare/v2.0.0...v2.0.1  <details> <summary><b>简体中文</b></summary>  ## 升级前须知  - **开启 TUN 时，Clash 设置中选择的 TUN 堆栈现在优先于 Profile 中的 `tun.stack`。** 以前订阅写入的堆栈会覆盖设置。如果你依赖订阅指定堆栈，请改在设置中选择（默认为 gVisor）。 - **Profile 设置了当前内核不支持的 TUN 堆栈时，配置现在会生成失败，错误信息会列出该值与内核支持的值。** 例如在 Clash Premium 下写 `tun.stack: mixed` 或 `mips`，否则内核会拒绝加载整份配置。由于内核加载配置时就会解析这个键，TUN 关闭时也会检查。Nyanpasu 不认识的值仍会原样交给内核。 - **堆栈选择器只列出当前内核会读取的堆栈**，clash-rs 与 Meow 不读取该键，因此不再显示选择器。如果保存的堆栈不被切换后的内核支持，运行时改用 gVisor 并在日志中记录警告，保存的设置不变。 - **服务模式现在需要 nyanpasu-service 2.0.2。** 已安装的 2.0.1 服务会在启动时自动升级一次。2.0.2 修复了残留的 pid 记录导致服务与应用每次启动都失败的问题（#6033）。  ## 主要变更  - **Mips（Mihomo IP Stack）**：mihomo 新增的用户态 TUN 堆栈，可在 mihomo 与 mihomo-alpha 下选择，并可设置 TCP 拥塞控制算法（CUBIC、Reno、BBR、BBRv3 或内核默认）。切换到其他堆栈再切回时，该设置会保留（#6091）。 - **启动失败不再导致应用崩溃。** 对话框会说明出错的文件、原因与恢复步骤，并可打开所在目录、备份目录或日志目录。损坏的本地数据与不可用的任务日志会被重建，不再阻止启动。 - 修复了 Logo 动画边缘、滚动时页面标题与流量迷你图拉伸的显示问题；更新链接与顶部菜单对齐。 - Windows 安装包随 enableLoopback 附带 Windows App SDK 引导程序。  完整的变更列表见上方英文部分。  </details> 
```

### 2026-11-01T07:33:37Z [**lollipopkit/flutter_server_box**](https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box)

```
### Added  - **Every block device in the server details:** the disk dialog lists all disks and partitions, including unmounted and blank disks, with model, size, type, connection and file system. Also in the Monitor panel (agent v0.3.0). Linux only. - **Android:** a redesigned home-screen widget that can be resized and shows more as it grows; Home-screen shortcuts to a server; a Quick Settings tile for sessions; share text and files from other apps into a snippet or a server; keyboard shortcuts; the app's language in system settings; and widget previews in the launcher. - **Editing files on Monitor-only servers and this device,** as on SSH servers. A file that changed on the server since it was opened is not overwritten without asking. - Persian (فارسی), the first right-to-left language.  ### Changed  - AI settings are a top-level item in Settings. - The Wiki link is removed from About and the macOS menu. - Translations revised in all languages, including the Android widget, the store listing, the Monitor panel and the website. - Monitor agent v0.3.0: ntex 4.  ### Fixed  - The editor: select-all with an input method, and moving the cursor across lines. - The "clear all settings" button no longer appears on every settings sub-page, where it could reset the language and the first-run setup. - WebDAV restore no longer fails when a file name contains a `%`. - Android: stopping a session on purpose is no longer treated as a lost connection. - Monitor agent v0.3.0 on Windows: the agent no longer crashes about 20 seconds after starting as a service or over SSH; a command that times out is ended with all its child processes.  中文更新日志：https://serverbox.lollipopkit.com/docs/zh/changelog/  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box/compare/v1.0.1844...v1.0.1856 
```

### 2026-10-31T11:24:51Z [**YunaiV/yudao-cloud**](https://github.com/YunaiV/yudao-cloud)

本月概要： * 【信创】扩展国产数据库接入，补充国产应用服务器与缓存中间件的兼容、接入说明。 * 【数据库】完善 PostgreSQL、Oracle、SQL Server 等数据库的 SQL 兼容与初始化脚本。 * 【移动端】IM 音视频通话支持 App、微信小程序接入，完善 Android、iOS 运行与打包发布说明。 * 【IoT】新增设备影子、消息透传与 JavaScript 数据解析，支持 IoTDB 存储，完善设备状态管理。 * 【管理后台】Vue2 完成 19 个业务模块对齐，新增手机确认扫码登录，优化多套管理后台的首屏加载与交互体验。 * 【部署与集成】补充 Docker Compose、Kubernetes、Jenkins 部署说明，提供分库分表、分布式事务及限流熔断的可选接入，增加 RustFS 存储配置。 * 【稳定性】修复工作流、支付、商城、CRM、MES 等模块的用户反馈问题，完善权限校验、文件下载安全及大整数编号处理。  ### 📈 Statistic  * 总代码行数：712996 * 源码代码行数：460703 * 注释行数：150116 * 单元测试用例数：5787  ### 🚀 新增功能  后端框架专区： * 【优化】完善达梦数据库安装说明，建议从官网下载较新的 DM8 服务端版本，增加完整版本和 `FIND_IN_SET` 可用性检查，并说明 Docker 镜像示例及已有数据的升级注意事项，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 提供文档，详见 《国产信创数据库》[单体文档](https://doc.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/)、[微服务文档](https://cloud.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/) * 【增强】完善瀚高 HighGo 的接入：瀚高原生 JDBC 驱动（`jdbc:highgo:`）按 PostgreSQL 识别，自动适配主键策略、Quartz（`master-jdk17` 分支）；明确 JDBC 驱动需要显式引入（单体在 `yudao-server` 中，微服务在每个连接数据库的服务中；`yudao-dependencies` 只管理版本），补充微服务各服务及 Nacos 的数据源配置、找不到 Driver 的排查；`sql/tools` 的 `highgo` 服务改为默认不启动，新增空库初始化入口 `highgo-init`；已在瀚高 6.0.4 上使用 PostgreSQL、瀚高两种驱动完成组件级验证，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 提供配置与文档，详见 《国产信创数据库》[单体文档](https://doc.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_4-highgo-瀚高数据库)、[微服务文档](https://cloud.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_4-highgo-瀚高数据库)，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/xBrgq> * 【新增】增加 TiDB 数据库的可选接入，默认仍使用 MySQL：直接复用 MySQL 初始化脚本和 JDBC 驱动，`sql/tools` 新增默认不启动的 TiDB v8.5.8 测试集群（PD、TiKV、TiDB）及空库初始化入口，并说明微服务各服务及 Nacos 的数据源配置、事务与兼容边界，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 提供配置与文档，详见 《国产信创数据库》[单体文档](https://doc.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_6-tidb-数据库)、[微服务文档](https://cloud.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_6-tidb-数据库) * 【新增】增加华为 GaussDB、云和恩墨 MogDB、海量 Vastbase G100、PolarDB 的可选接入：GaussDB、MogDB、Vastbase 使用 PG 兼容模式，基于 openGauss 初始化脚本导入，GaussDB、openGauss 驱动按 PostgreSQL 识别，自动适配主键策略、Quartz、Flowable；PolarDB PostgreSQL 版直接复用 PostgreSQL 脚本和驱动；`sql/tools` 新增 GaussDB 建库入口、默认不启动的 行锁语法，统一单条、批量及首条锁定读，保留租户、数据权限和结果映射；锁定读需在事务内调用。单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】Oracle、人大金仓按业务表适配保留字列引用，与转换后的列名保持一致，并保留租户、数据权限、分页及结果映射；不自动修改数据库结构。单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】完成关系型数据库通用 SQL 兼容的首批修复：统计空值与条件表达式、MES 原子数量增量、整数逻辑删除、分组和结果别名映射、表别名、UNION 空值类型及缺表异常识别；本批在单体实现，微服务共享部分按后续同步处理，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 贡献，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/iGGfQ> * 【修复】统计查询按实际数据源适配日期分桶、月份与生日筛选，补齐 Oracle 标量汇总语法，保留原日期返回格式；成交周期统计适配 PostgreSQL、Oracle、SQL Server，按完整天数计算并保留一位小数（截断）；单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/iGGfQ> * 【修复】适配排行查询及日志、Token 分批清理的数据库差异；统一分页工具和限量查询入口，限量查询不再查询总数，普通查询改用 Lambda 字段引用，保留原筛选、排序与查询字段。自定义 Mapper 中的旧 `QueryWrapperX.limitN` 调用需迁移至 `BaseMapperX.selectListLimitN`。单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 贡献，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/iGGfQ> * 【修复】修正 SQL Server 转换器与新装脚本的字段类型：`bit(1)` 使用原生 `bit`，有符号 `tinyint`、`smallint` 使用 `smallint`，保留负数及较大整数；补齐无符号小整数映射。本批在单体实现，微服务共享部分按后续同步处理；已有数据库不自动改列，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】短信验证码 `used` 字段使用公共整数布尔类型转换器，Java 保持 Boolean，数据库按整数 0/1 读写，兼容 PostgreSQL 等数据库；转换器按字段启用，保留原生 boolean/bit 字段的处理。本批在单体实现，微服务共享部分按后续同步处理，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】会议室指定可预定人员、工作项标签筛选适配 PostgreSQL、Oracle、SQL Server、人大金仓、瀚高和 H2；保留数值编号精度、标签 OR 条件及分页前过滤，MySQL 查询和参数类型保持不变。单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】商城支付状态改为布尔参数绑定，修复 CRM 客户画像统计和 MES 待检查询在 PostgreSQL 下的布尔类型错误，保留空值聚合语义与 OQC ### 2026-10-31T11:24:38Z [**YunaiV/ruoyi-vue-pro**](https://github.com/YunaiV/ruoyi-vue-pro)

本月概要： * 【信创】扩展国产数据库接入，补充国产应用服务器与缓存中间件的兼容、接入说明。 * 【数据库】完善 PostgreSQL、Oracle、SQL Server 等数据库的 SQL 兼容与初始化脚本。 * 【移动端】IM 音视频通话支持 App、微信小程序接入，完善 Android、iOS 运行与打包发布说明。 * 【IoT】新增设备影子、消息透传与 JavaScript 数据解析，支持 IoTDB 存储，完善设备状态管理。 * 【管理后台】Vue2 完成 19 个业务模块对齐，新增手机确认扫码登录，优化多套管理后台的首屏加载与交互体验。 * 【部署与集成】补充 Docker Compose、Kubernetes、Jenkins 部署说明，提供分库分表、分布式事务及限流熔断的可选接入，增加 RustFS 存储配置。 * 【稳定性】修复工作流、支付、商城、CRM、MES 等模块的用户反馈问题，完善权限校验、文件下载安全及大整数编号处理。  ### 📈 Statistic  * 总代码行数：712996 * 源码代码行数：460703 * 注释行数：150116 * 单元测试用例数：5787  ### 🚀 新增功能  后端框架专区： * 【优化】完善达梦数据库安装说明，建议从官网下载较新的 DM8 服务端版本，增加完整版本和 `FIND_IN_SET` 可用性检查，并说明 Docker 镜像示例及已有数据的升级注意事项，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 提供文档，详见 《国产信创数据库》[单体文档](https://doc.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/)、[微服务文档](https://cloud.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/) * 【增强】完善瀚高 HighGo 的接入：瀚高原生 JDBC 驱动（`jdbc:highgo:`）按 PostgreSQL 识别，自动适配主键策略、Quartz（`master-jdk17` 分支）；明确 JDBC 驱动需要显式引入（单体在 `yudao-server` 中，微服务在每个连接数据库的服务中；`yudao-dependencies` 只管理版本），补充微服务各服务及 Nacos 的数据源配置、找不到 Driver 的排查；`sql/tools` 的 `highgo` 服务改为默认不启动，新增空库初始化入口 `highgo-init`；已在瀚高 6.0.4 上使用 PostgreSQL、瀚高两种驱动完成组件级验证，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 提供配置与文档，详见 《国产信创数据库》[单体文档](https://doc.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_4-highgo-瀚高数据库)、[微服务文档](https://cloud.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_4-highgo-瀚高数据库)，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/xBrgq> * 【新增】增加 TiDB 数据库的可选接入，默认仍使用 MySQL：直接复用 MySQL 初始化脚本和 JDBC 驱动，`sql/tools` 新增默认不启动的 TiDB v8.5.8 测试集群（PD、TiKV、TiDB）及空库初始化入口，并说明微服务各服务及 Nacos 的数据源配置、事务与兼容边界，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 提供配置与文档，详见 《国产信创数据库》[单体文档](https://doc.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_6-tidb-数据库)、[微服务文档](https://cloud.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_6-tidb-数据库) * 【新增】增加华为 GaussDB、云和恩墨 MogDB、海量 Vastbase G100、PolarDB 的可选接入：GaussDB、MogDB、Vastbase 使用 PG 兼容模式，基于 openGauss 初始化脚本导入，GaussDB、openGauss 驱动按 PostgreSQL 识别，自动适配主键策略、Quartz、Flowable；PolarDB PostgreSQL 版直接复用 PostgreSQL 脚本和驱动；`sql/tools` 新增 GaussDB 建库入口、默认不启动的 MogDB 测试服务，并说明微服务各服务及 Nacos 的数据源配置；已在 GaussDB 507、MogDB 5、PolarDB PostgreSQL 17、Vastbase G100 V2.2 上完成组件级验证，暂不支持 PolarDB-X、PolarDB Oracle 兼容模式，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 提供配置与文档，详见 《国产信创数据库》[单体文档](https://doc.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_7-华为-gaussdb-数据库)、[微服务文档](https://cloud.iocoder.cn/xinchuang-db/#_7-华为-gaussdb-数据库) * 【新增】增加神通数据库支持（`master-jdk17` 分支，默认仍使用 MySQL），覆盖普通 `com.shentongdata:oscarJDBC8`，并说明微服务各服务及 Nacos 的数据源配置；Quartz 需按文档将 `bytea` 字段改为 `BLOB` 后全新初始化（微服务在 `iot-server` 的库中），不自动转换已有数据；Flowable 自动按 `oracle` 创建引擎表，BPM 业务 SQL 需另行导入，由 行锁语法，统一单条、批量及首条锁定读，保留租户、数据权限和结果映射；锁定读需在事务内调用。单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】Oracle、人大金仓按业务表适配保留字列引用，与转换后的列名保持一致，并保留租户、数据权限、分页及结果映射；不自动修改数据库结构。单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】完成关系型数据库通用 SQL 兼容的首批修复：统计空值与条件表达式、MES 原子数量增量、整数逻辑删除、分组和结果别名映射、表别名、UNION 空值类型及缺表异常识别；本批在单体实现，微服务共享部分按后续同步处理，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 贡献，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/iGGfQ> * 【修复】统计查询按实际数据源适配日期分桶、月份与生日筛选，补齐 Oracle 标量汇总语法，保留原日期返回格式；成交周期统计适配 PostgreSQL、Oracle、SQL Server，按完整天数计算并保留一位小数（截断）；单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复，对应 <https://t.zsxq.com/iGGfQ> * 【修复】适配排行查询及日志、Token 分批清理的数据库差异；统一分页工具和限量查询入口，限量查询不再查询总数，普通查询改用 Lambda 字段引用，保留原筛选、排序与查询字段。自定义 Mapper 中的旧 `QueryWrapperX.limitN` 调用需迁移至 `BaseMapperX.selectListLimitN`。单体已实现，微服务共享部分待后续同步，由 `smallint`，保留负数及较大整数；补齐无符号小整数映射。本批在单体实现，微服务共享部分按后续同步处理；已有数据库不自动改列，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】短信验证码 `used` 字段使用公共整数布尔类型转换器，Java 保持 Boolean，数据库按整数 0/1 读写，兼容 PostgreSQL 等数据库；转换器按字段启用，保留原生 boolean/bit 字段的处理。本批在单体实现，微服务共享部分按后续同步处理，由 [@芋道源码](https://gitee.com/zhijiantianya) 修复 * 【修复】会议室指定可预定人员、工作项标签筛选适配 PostgreSQL、Oracle、SQL Server、人大金仓、瀚高和 H2；保留数值编号精度、标签 OR ### 2026-11-01T05:53:04Z [**ChatGPTBox-dev/chatGPTBox**](https://github.com/ChatGPTBox-dev/chatGPTBox)

```
This maintenance release adds new API model options and improves floating conversations, streaming, and interaction reliability.  ## Changes  ### New Models  - Add OpenAI GPT-6.1 Solm Terra, and GPT-6 Sol, Luna API options. - Add Anthropic Claude Opus 5.5, Claude Sonnet 5.5, and Claude Haiku 5.5 API options. - Refresh the default-enabled API model list and model-specific request parameters while preserving existing selections and older model options.  ### Improvements and Fixes  - Improve floating chat layout and model selection in smaller windows, including viewport-aware sizing, drag recovery, and repositioning after resize (#1096). - Decode SSE response streams incrementally across split UTF-8 chunks and clear parser metadata on reset (#1068, #1069). - Clean up theme and selection-tool listeners, discard stale asynchronous work, and handle selection prompt failures (#1066, #1067, #1079, #1081, #1093). - Dispose of conversation-owned connections on close or unmount and invalidate pending work after disconnect or Stop (#1082, #1083). - Upgrade KaTeX while preserving the layout of `\smash` expressions (#1114).  ### Maintenance  - Update dependencies to address reported security issues and add related regression coverage. - Add dedicated formatting and lint checks, and pin GitHub Actions to immutable commits. - Separate Chrome Web Store publishing using API v2 so its failures do not block GitHub releases or Firefox and Edge submissions (#1091).  **Contributors:** @PeterDaveHello, @hamzahamidi  **Full Changelog**: [v2.7.1...v2.7.2](https://github.com/ChatGPTBox-dev/chatGPTBox/compare/v2.7.1...v2.7.2) 
```

### 2026-10-28T22:03:41Z [**pwndbg/pwndbg**](https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg)

```
_This is a hotfix release for the [2026.10.22](https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg/releases/tag/2026.10.22) release._  **This release fixes Pwndbg for LLDB, which did not start at all in 2026.10.22 portable builds.** If you installed `pwndbg-lldb` 2026.10.22 (via the install script, Homebrew, or the release packages and tarballs), please upgrade. Pwndbg for GDB was not affected.  It also brings a few fixes to the `spray`, `hexdump` and `try-free` commands.  Thanks to everyone who reported the issue and contributed!  Please [sponsor us at Pwndbg](https://github.com/sponsors/pwndbg) so we can keep improving it! -- We can give you a talk about Pwndbg too!  ----  ## Quick install  Install Pwndbg portable on Linux or macOS (choose one line for GDB or LLDB):  sh curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -LsSf 'https://install.pwndbg.re' | sh -s -- -t pwndbg-gdb curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -LsSf 'https://install.pwndbg.re' | sh -s -- -t pwndbg-lldb   Homebrew package (macOS only; choose one line for GDB or LLDB):  sh brew install pwndbg/tap/pwndbg-gdb  # only allows for remote debugging of ELFs brew install pwndbg/tap/pwndbg-lldb   Nix package manager (Linux/macOS; choose one line for GDB or LLDB):  sh nix shell github:pwndbg/pwndbg  # this is pwndbg-gdb nix shell github:pwndbg/pwndbg#pwndbg-lldb   Other options:  * For distro and architecture packages, see the [setup docs](https://pwndbg.re/stable/setup) and the release assets below. * For the latest development version, [install from source](https://pwndbg.re/dev/setup/#installing-from-source).  ----  ## What's changed  ### LLDB regression fix  In 2026.10.22, the portable `pwndbg-lldb` failed to start with:   pwndbg-lldb: line 3: .../pwndbg/bin/python3: No such file or directory   The launcher script was changed to run `bin/python3` (#4102), but that wrapper was only added to the GDB bundle and not to the LLDB one. The LLDB bundle now includes it too ([#4268](https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg/pull/4268)).  ### Other fixes  * `spray <addr> <length> --value <value>` now writes the value (truncated) when the length is shorter than the value; previously it wrote nothing ([#4261](https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg/pull/4261)) * `hexdump` no longer hides the first line of a run of identical lines that comes right after exactly two identical lines ([#4260](https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg/pull/4260)) * `try-free` now checks the unsorted bin the same way glibc does, so it catches the `free(): corrupted unsorted chunks` case instead of reporting "All checks passed!" ([#4262](https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg/pull/4262))  ---  Full Changelog: https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg/compare/2026.10.22...2026.10.28  Thanks also to our new contributor: @sindooo ([#4268](https://github.com/pwndbg/pwndbg/pull/4268)), who found and fixed the LLDB regression! 
```

### 2026-11-02T04:29:21Z [**miniflux/v2**](https://github.com/miniflux/v2)

```
 ### New features  - Added OpenID Connect admin role mapping: set `OAUTH2_ADMIN_GROUP` and `OAUTH2_GROUP_CLAIM` to grant or revoke the admin role at login based on the user's groups. The group claim is read from the user info response or the ID token, and `OAUTH2_ADDITIONAL_SCOPES` can request extra scopes from the provider. - Allowed WebAuthn/Passkeys as the only login method when `DISABLE_LOCAL_AUTH` is enabled. Register at least one passkey while another authentication method is still available before turning off local authentication. - Added a `force` parameter to the feed refresh API endpoint (`PUT /v1/feeds/{id}/refresh?force=true`) to bypass the HTTP cache and reprocess existing entries, matching the forced refresh in the web UI. The Go client gains `ForceRefreshFeed`. - Rejected invalid CSS selectors when saving rewrite and scraper rules, instead of silently ignoring them. - Moved the username, password, OAuth2 account linking, and passkey settings to a dedicated **Authentication** page under Settings.  ### Performance improvements  - Stopped creating database web sessions for anonymous visitors, crawlers, media proxy requests, and other public pages that don't need one. Logged-out language and theme preferences are now kept in a cookie, and anonymous sessions are removed after 24 hours, which keeps the `web_sessions` table from growing with thousands of unused rows.  ### Security  - Required the current password before setting a new one on the Authentication page. - Signed out other sessions when a password is changed, from the web UI, the API, or the `-reset-password` command. The current session gets a new identifier so a copied cookie stops working. - Prevented administrators from editing their own account through the user management page, which bypassed the current password check. - Restricted feed cookies to the feed's own website, so they are no longer sent to third-party hosts when scraping articles, fetching icons, or discovering feeds. - Blocked 6to4 and IPv4-compatible IPv6 addresses that could bypass the server-side request forgery filter. - Stopped caching shared entry pages in public caches and removed the CSRF token from them. - Took the client IP from the right side of `X-Forwarded-For`, skipping addresses in `TRUSTED_REVERSE_PROXY_NETWORKS`, so clients can no longer spoof the IP recorded in logs and sessions. **If you chain several reverse proxies (e.g. Cloudflare in front of nginx), add the networks of every proxy to `TRUSTED_REVERSE_PROXY_NETWORKS`.** - Stopped logging malformed Google Reader authentication tokens. - Hardened the GitHub Actions workflows and added provenance and SBOM attestations to Docker images.  ### Bug fixes  - Fixed the Fever API toggling the starred state when saving or unsaving an entry that was already in that state. - Fixed the Fever API marking all entries as read, including newer ones, while ignoring the `before` timestamp. - Fixed the Fever API returning an arbitrary set of entries for some item requests, which could cause clients to miss entries. - Fixed a crash in the Google Reader API caused by an unknown stream type. - Matched category titles case-insensitively, fixing "category not found" errors with Google Reader labels and duplicate categories created by OPML imports. - Parsed RFC 3339 dates with a lower-case `t` or `z`. - Accepted JSON feeds that start with a UTF-8 byte order mark. - Validated the username format when renaming a user, not only at creation.  ### Localization  - Updated Polish (`pl_PL`) translations. - Fixed raw translation keys shown instead of messages for invalid usernames, invalid proxy URLs, unparsable HTML pages, and the tag entry count.  ### Documentation  - Reviewed the man page for accuracy and readability, documenting option combinations checked at startup, media proxy modes, and systemd socket activation.  ### Dependencies  - Updated several Go modules and GitHub Actions dependencies.  ---  As always, thank you to all contributors who helped improve Miniflux in this release. 
```
